**Latest update (live-strategy-sync-and-watchlist, 2026-10-10):** LIVE UI STRATEGY SYNCHRONIZATION & DYNAMIC WATCHLIST COMPLETE (local only, no commit/push).
1. Central Strategy Selection: `LiveTradingService.available_strategies()` integrated directly with central `StrategyRegistry`, returning canonical strategy IDs (`d.id`) rather than ad-hoc display names. Resolves configuration robustly without hardcoded lists.
2. Automatic NSE Stock Synchronization: Directly connects Strategy Lab's saved per-strategy universes via `StrategyUniverseStore` (`live/strategy_universes.json`). Switching strategies loads saved NSE universe immediately; switching back restores it.
3. Functional Watchlist & Ghost Row Elimination:
   - In `crates/vayren-domain/src/live.rs`, `select_strategy(index)` clears `self.watchlist_rows` and `self.selected_symbol` immediately to eliminate 1-frame ghost displays.
   - `apply_snapshot` assigns `self.watchlist_rows = wl_rows;` unconditionally, ensuring an empty universe (`quotes: []`) properly clears previous rows.
   - Quotes enriched with real `order` and `pnl` from `LiveSession.ledger` and `ExecutionEngine`, plus real stop levels from `session._stop_levels`. Sizing derives strictly from `CapitalRiskEngine` and verified capital.
4. Non-Invasive Safety: Selecting a strategy never changes state from `STOPPED`, never arms execution (`armed = False`), and never submits orders.
5. Verification: All 147 tests in `src/app/tests/` passed including new comprehensive test `test_live_strategy_sync_and_watchlist.py` (5/5 passed); Rust tests `empty_quotes_in_snapshot_clears_watchlist_rows` and `select_strategy_clears_stale_checks_and_forwards` passed (256 domain tests green); all 6 repository governance validators PASS 100%. Report written to `docs/live/live-strategy-sync-and-watchlist-report.md`.

4. Live: `available_symbols()` / `symbols_for()` return only the selected strategy's saved universe (no declaration or market-store fallback). Live watchlist = saved universe; configuring never starts trading.

**Previous update (obr-strategy-optimization, 2026-10-10):** OBR STRATEGY ONLY MANDATORY OPTIMIZATION & VERIFIED PARITY COMPLETE (local only, no commit/push).
1. Strict Scope & Boundaries: Optimized exclusively the authoritative OBR strategy in `D:/VAYREN_STRATEGIES/OBR.py` and its direct backtest path. Preserved 100% of OBR Pine Script layer rules (0–12), parameters (`refIndex=3`, `extendBars=5`), entry/exit signals, and trade execution semantics.
2. Hotspots Identified & Fixed:
   - Per-bar timestamp parsing in `_engine_bar`: Replaced expensive `datetime.fromisoformat()` and `.replace(tzinfo=IST)` with optimized integer date and time components, computing `is_exit_time` directly during Bar creation.
   - Headless backtest plot bypass: Added conditional gating in `_plot_obr_visuals` and `plot_ray` (`getattr(self, "_plot_enabled", False)`) so that `PlotEvent` allocations are bypassed when running batch backtests without active chart listeners.
   - Market day and trade collection: Added `_sharedMarketDays_set` for O(1) set membership during day registration; equipped `Bar` and `TradeRecord` with `__slots__`.
3. Measured Performance & Parity:
   - 10-stock warm compute time: **20.14s -> 12.17s** (**39.6% faster**, 1.65x throughput).
   - Trade count parity: **7,781 trades** (baseline) vs **7,781 trades** (optimized) — **100% Exact Match**.
   - Net profit parity: **-98,999.58** vs **-98,999.58** — **100% Exact Match**.
4. 526-Stock Universe Scalability Analysis:
   - Full 526 stocks comprise 24,719,146 historical bars. In single-threaded CPython, raw SQLite I/O + object instantiation requires ~450–600s, and state machine evaluation requires ~494s. Achieving <60s requires >412,000 bars/sec throughput, which mathematically requires a native Rust OBR kernel (`crates/vayren-core`) directly reading SQLite or memory-mapped buffers.
5. Verification: All gates green (`make check-fast` clean; `pytest src/app/tests/test_backtest_service.py` 11/11 passed; `pytest src/strategy/tests` 206/206 passed; `cargo check -p vayren-shell --lib` passed; `validate_imports`, `validate_structure`, `validate_language_ownership` passed on 393 files). Full report at `docs/performance/obr-strategy-optimization-report.md`.

**Previous update (phase3-slint-backtest-opt, 2026-10-10):** PHASE 3 SLINT CODEGEN, CROSS-CRATE & BACKTEST OPTIMIZATION COMPLETE (local only, no commit/push).
1. Slint Compilation Architecture: Profiled root cause of 51s rebuild latency (31.6 MB generated `app.rs` in `vayren-shell`). Established modular view crate workflow (`vayren-*-view`): compiling dedicated view targets yields 2.3x to 4.5x faster incremental checks (e.g., `vayren-portfolio-view` check in 11.42s vs 50.98s for the shell monolith).
2. Cross-Crate Propagation: Traced why domain crate changes propagate to shell checks in 45.48s: rustc typechecks `app.rs` AST with `shell.rs`, but Slint codegen is avoided via content hashing.
3. Indicator & Backtest Optimization: Discovered and optimized `calc_sma` and `calc_range` in `src/strategy/strategies/indicators.py` to use `islice(reversed(...))` instead of allocating full Python lists per bar (5.13x faster on bar loops: 0.27s -> 0.05s). 10-symbol backtest accelerated to 12.96s (8,163 trades).
4. Full Universe Profiling: Profiled 526 stocks containing 24,719,146 historical OHLCV bars. Quantified single-thread Python limits (~680s total execution) and defined path forward toward native Rust execution kernels.
5. Verification: All gates green (`make check-fast` 5.00s; 403 core tests, 247 domain tests, 49 shell tests, 115 app tests, 196 strategy tests, 197 broker tests pass; repo graph 5733 entities 0 unresolved). Reports written to `docs/performance/phase3-optimization-report.md` and `docs/performance/phase3-benchmarks.json`.

**Latest update (strategy-lab-perf-polish, 2026-10-09):** STRATEGY LAB POLISH + SPEED — window-origin bug fixed, scroll fast-path, backtest bar cache (local only, no commit/push).
1. **REAL BUG — virtual-window row offset.** `lab.slint` positioned a band row at `50 + rindex*row_h - scroll_px`, but `rindex` is BAND-relative while `scroll_px` is DATASET-absolute: every scrolled row sat `first * row_h` too high, so a deep scroll rendered a blank band. Ranking + trade blotter both fixed to `(window.first + index)`. Data-side pin: new domain test `rank_window_first_anchors_every_visible_row` (rendered row `rindex` carries dataset `first + rindex` at EVERY offset).
2. **Ranking lag — scroll rebuilt the whole screen.** Every wheel notch ran full `apply_lab` (~500 lines: library, KPIs, presets, equity, detail models → re-instantiated rows + full relayout per tick). New `apply_lab_rank_scroll` / `apply_lab_trade_scroll`: window prop always, row model only on signature change; the 5 scroll/viewport handlers use them; band helpers now SHARED with `apply_lab` (one implementation, no drift). Guard: new `tests/lab_rank_scroll_fast.rs` (scroll-to(max) lands exactly, pushed band == projected window, unrelated models untouched). Bench evidence `perf_table` unchanged: project 0.13–0.17ms, scroll+project 0.19ms mean, 50k rows render 12–26 rows.
3. **Backtest speed (measured, real 40-symbol run on the 526-symbol store).** Profile showed load ≈ 50% (`get_bars` 4.5s of 8.6s): Bar construction per run, dict-dedup + O(n log n) re-sort per read. Now (a) LRU bars cache keyed on (symbol, timeframe, limit, start, end, DB mtime) — identical re-RUN returns the same immutable tuple; measured **cold 3.5s → repeat 0.7s (4.9x)**, results byte-identical (asserted); (b) `_read_rows` builds in SQL ASC/DESC order (dedup is keep-first, insertion order == sorted) — the re-sort is gone, DESC tail reversed once; (c) one-pass per-symbol P&L, `_rank_row` symbol filter removed (`_run_symbol` only appends its own symbol). Full 526-symbol cold run still ~30s (strategy Python loop is the floor; ~0.28ms/1000 bars, `indicators.calc_sma` re-sums the whole window each bar — Python-owned per AI_ENTRY §1).
4. **BUG IN MY OWN LAST PUSH — fixed here.** The Slint content-hash guard (37b0cc2) skipped `slint_build::compile` WITHOUT emitting `cargo:rustc-env=SLINT_INCLUDE_GENERATED`, which `slint::include_modules!()` reads at compile time: the next warm build failed with "environment variable not defined" + the E0432 import cascade. Verified locally (broke exactly once, then fixed). The skip path now prints the same env line pointing at the same generated file. Lesson: a cache-skip branch must reproduce EVERY observable side effect of the path it replaces — I diffed "file bytes" and missed "build protocol".
5. Verified: `cargo test -p vayren-shell` all suites green (49 lib + render/lab/scroll suites); `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib` 241/241; `cargo fmt` clean; pyright 0 errors on the touched Python; ruff clean on them; validators + graph PASS (5381 entities).
6. **Pre-existing, NOT mine** (concurrent uncommitted work in the same tree): `src/strategy/tests/test_eligibility.py` 13 failures (assertion-level in the new eligibility engine) + ruff I001 in `src/strategy/__init__.py` — untouched on purpose.
7. Transplant note: PowerShell `Get-Content -Raw` mangled UTF-8 (em-dash → `â€"`) and nearly committed mojibake; all UTF-8 transplants now go through Python.

**Latest update (fast-market-ui-polish-2, 2026-10-09):** FAST MARKET UI POLISH 2 — the market-status strip finally paints + truncation/pointer/empty-state polish (local only, no commit/push). Files: `crates/vayren-shell/ui/market.slint`, NEW `crates/vayren-shell/tests/market_status_strip.rs`, `docs/repo_graph.json` (rebuilt). Zero Rust/Python product logic changed.
1. **THE MISSING SCREEN — the market-status strip was wired end-to-end and never rendered.** Rust had it all: `MarketStatusFacts` (regime_current/trend/volatility/momentum + provider/latency/last_update/bars_loaded, honest `--` defaults), `MarketAction::ToggleStatus` (view-local, no backend wire), `market_status_rows()` splitting 8 facts into MARKET REGIME + DATA STATUS, `project()` emitting `market_status_open/regime/data`, `apply_market` (shell.rs:487-500) and `vayren-market-view` (lib.rs:238-251) binding all three, and BOTH hosts (`app.slint:732-734` + `market_host.slint:214-217`) wiring the properties and `status-toggle() => market-status-toggle() => MarketAction::ToggleStatus`. `market.slint` declared `market-status-open` / `-regime` / `-data` / `status-toggle()` (lines 1744-1747) and then never read a single one — the legacy `MarketStatusPanel` parity claim in the struct comment was false, and the whole panel was unreachable. **Rendered now:** rail gains a third `RailButton { code: "MS" }` (the domain doc for `ToggleStatus` says "rail button"; WL/DL conventions kept), and the strip sizes by content at the bottom of the chart column — two `StatusGroup`s (MARKET REGIME + DATA STATUS, faint 11px eyebrows) of `StatusFact` cells (caption above value, `--` muted per the honest-unknown rule) behind a 1px hairline-soft separator. No explicit height, so the chart keeps priority and loses nothing while collapsed.
2. **Regression test that proves pixels, not bindings:** NEW `tests/market_status_strip.rs` renders the REAL `AppWindow` (software rasterizer, chart screen, real `apply_snapshot_json` payload) four times: closed → open → closed again → honest-unknown → medium tier. Asserts the strip's own surface band grows by >20k px, fact glyphs and captions paint ONLY when open (closed frame must stay clean), the strip disappears when toggled back off (not latched), `--` rows still paint, and it survives 1024x640. Closed-frame vs open-frame delta is the guard: a strip that stays invisible fails on the first assert. 1/1 PASS.
3. **Truncation honesty (9 sites, values only):** `overflow: elide` added where a long string could clip mid-glyph — MTimeButton label (the open item polish-1 left behind), SmallButton label ("Configure Credentials" / "OPEN IN MARK…"), SettingsField label, CredFieldRow label, PopupItem section + empty (also given the item row's explicit `width: parent.width - 16px`, without which they overflowed instead of eliding), and DlKVRow value as `overflow: root.wrap ? TextOverflow.clip : TextOverflow.elide` (wrap rows keep wrapping; single-line rows truncate instead of clipping). The five trade-context Texts got `min-width: 0px; overflow: elide` so a long trade string can no longer push PREV / NEXT / OPEN IN MARKET off the strip.
4. **Pointer affordance:** the plot, price-strip and time-axis interaction surfaces (pan / zoom / drag / double-click reset) plus the timeframe tab row and the FROM/TO `DlField` boxes (which open the calendar on click) had NO cursor at all — the three chart surfaces now show `crosshair`, the two clickable controls show `pointer`. Every TouchArea in the file now answers the pointer except the two invisible click-to-dismiss scrims behind the floating menus, where a cursor would advertise a target that is not a control.
5. **Empty watchlist is no longer a silent void:** the panel rendered literally nothing for an empty list. It now prints the honest cause, chosen from UI-local state: `"No symbols match the filter."` vs `"No symbols in this list."` (mirrors live.slint:1219-1228's empty-list pattern).
6. **Verified (all real):** `cargo check -p vayren-shell --lib` PASS (3m21s, zero warnings on market.slint); `cargo test -p vayren-shell` **68/68** (49 lib + 3 doc + 7 lab_config_render + 1 lab_scroll + 1 lab_select + 4 live_render_snapshot + 1 NEW market_status_strip + 1 render_app + 1 render_matrix); `cargo test -p vayren-market-view` 13/13 (2 lib + 11 render_market); `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib` 240/241 (1 pre-existing ignore); `cargo fmt -p vayren-shell -- --check` clean; `validate_structure` / `validate_language_ownership` (372 files) / `validate_imports` / `validate_authority` (223 py, 19 slint, 19 routes) / `validate_routes` PASS; `docs/repo_graph.json` REBUILT (5451 entities, 11265 relationships, 0 unresolved — required, Slint line numbers moved and that is what broke the `validators` CI job before).
7. **Pre-existing, NOT mine (untouched on purpose):** `validate_architecture_gate` fails on uncommitted FYERS files (`fyers_apiv3.FyersWebsocket.data_ws` / `.order_ws` third-party imports not declared in pyproject) — no Python was touched in this pass, so a full `make check` stays red until those land on their own branch.
8. **Open, deliberately NOT done (features, not polish):** the volume pane is dead-but-still-bound (`hover-volume` / `hover-vol-pos` / `hover-bull` feed two `visible: false` elements; removing them means editing app.slint + market_host.slint + the shell.rs setters, a 3-file refactor); the timeframe row still has no horizontal scroll at minimum width (elide now truncates honestly, but scrolling is the real fix); the rail has no tooltips, so `MS` is discoverable only by position; no keyboard shortcut for the strip (Alt+R reset is the only one).

**Latest update (strategy-lab-polish-pass, 2026-10-06):** STRATEGY LAB POLISH PASS - one real functional bug + honesty/affordance/layout fixes (local only, no commit/push). Files: `crates/vayren-shell/ui/lab.slint`, `crates/vayren-domain/src/lab.rs`, NEW `crates/vayren-shell/tests/lab_lens_pages.rs`, NEW `crates/vayren-shell/tests/lab_overlay_dialog.rs`. **Zero trading logic touched** (OBR C1C4, risk engine, sizing, broker routing untouched; no Python changed).
1. **THE BUG - two of five lens tabs rendered a blank page.** `lens == 3` (Drawdown) and `lens == 4` (Analytics) were written at the very END of `lab.slint`, nested inside the `if root.lab.sym-open:` universe-dialog block. The braces BALANCED, so Slint compiled, all validators passed, and the surface looked finished - but both lenses painted their only line of content into a 460px dialog `Rectangle` that exists solely while the universe picker is open. Selecting Drawdown or Analytics switched section 04 off and rendered nothing. Both are now page sections in the same flow as lens 0/1/2, each an eyebrow + a `panel` card stating what is missing rather than an invented curve. **Lesson worth keeping: brace balance is not nesting correctness - a depth trace found what the compiler and every validator could not.**
2. **Regression test written for it, and PROVEN to fail on the bug.** `lab_lens_pages.rs` selects each lens through the model's own `interaction_lens()` (a software-renderer harness cannot fire `TouchArea.clicked`) and compares pixels. With the bodies moved back inside the dialog: `drawdown vs analytics = 1762 px` -> both blank -> identical frames -> test FAILS. With the fix: `5299 px`, and each vs the Trades lens `~968k px` -> both paint a real body. Measured, not assumed.
3. **A painted lie removed - the dropdown caret on controls that open nothing.** `LabCtlButton` painted its `U+25BC` caret on EVERY instance, including the trade blotter's side / result / sort controls, which CYCLE their value inline and never open a menu; and the 26px trade-detail close button, which also computed a **negative label width** (`26 - 11 - 15 - 11 = -11px`) so the caret sat on top of the glyph. New `ctl-caret` property (default true) opts out per instance; the label reserves the caret gutter only when a caret is actually painted; the close button is now a plain icon button matching the universe dialog's own close.
4. **The first-run state said nothing at all.** The 44px "nothing to read yet" bar held only a stretch spacer plus a `+ New strategy` button gated on `name == ""` - so with a strategy selected and nothing run yet it rendered completely EMPTY. It now renders `lab.empty-reason`, the backend's own sentence ("No backtest results yet - run the backtest", "Results are outdated - run again", ...), which `lab.rs:2504` already computed and the screen never showed.
5. **Unknown is no longer painted as bad news.** MAX DRAWDOWN carried `es-tone: 3` unconditionally, so the *unknown* placeholder rendered RED - an alarm about a number nobody measured. Red is now earned by a measured value only. Also `"--"` -> em-dash in the OUTCOME and the equity footer stats, matching the dash the rest of the file uses. `studio_source` was a hardcoded `"LOCAL"` in `lab.rs` (a fabricated provenance reading as a measured fact); it now sends empty and the meta cell shows a dash like its three neighbours.
6. **Table clipping became truncation.** `LabTh`/`LabTd` never set `overflow`, and every metric column is `stretch: 1`, so a docked-width blotter hard-clipped dates and prices mid-glyph (`ENTRY PX`/`EXIT PX` had ~81px of text room for a ~110px string). Both now elide. The section 02 completeness strip's note width (`cfgcard.width * 60 / 100 - 415px`) went NEGATIVE below a ~692px card and silently vanished; it is clamped, then elided - the note degrades to "truncated", never to "absent".
7. **Three real buttons never changed the cursor** (mode cells, research-object rows, `+ New strategy`) while 29 others did - hover and press painted, pointer did not. Fixed. Token discipline: `LabPalette.fs-strat-name` / `fs-strat-name-sm` (44/34px) were defined and referenced NOWHERE while the same two numbers were re-typed literally; the literals now use the tokens (identical values, zero pixel change). Dead imports (`VayrenPalette`, `VayrenDesign`, `VBadge`, `VButton`, `VBanner`, `VEmptyState`, `VCell`) and a DUPLICATE `trade-needle` field declaration removed. `Palette` from std-widgets was nearly removed too - it IS used once in `init` (`Palette.color-scheme`), which the compiler caught.
8. **Verification (all real):** `cargo check -p vayren-shell` PASS with ZERO warnings pointing at `lab.slint` (the binding-loop warnings that remain are pre-existing, all in `live.slint` / `live_harness.slint`); `cargo test -p vayren-shell` **71/71 PASS** (53 lib + 7 `lab_config_render` + 1 `lab_lens_pages` + 1 `lab_overlay_dialog` + 1 `lab_scroll` + 1 `lab_select` + 2 `live_render_snapshot` + `render_app` + `render_matrix`); `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib` 236/236; `cargo fmt --all -- --check` clean; `validate_structure` / `validate_language_ownership` (330 files) / `validate_authority` (201 py, 19 slint, 17 routes) / `validate_routes` / `validate_repo_graph` / `context_engine --check` PASS. **`docs/repo_graph.json` REBUILT** (4769 entities, 8504 relationships, 0 unresolved) - required, because Slint line numbers moved and that is exactly what broke the `validators` CI job before.
9. **Not mine - PRE-EXISTING failures in this tree, from concurrent uncommitted FYERS/live work** (`live_market_data.py`, `session_adapter.py` are untracked/half-landed): `validate_imports` FAILS - `live_market_data.py:18,19` imports `execution.events` / `execution.market_data.provider` from `broker`, which `DOMAIN_DEPS` forbids; `validate_architecture_gate` FAILS - `fyers_apiv3.FyersWebsocket.data_ws` / `.order_ws` are third-party imports not declared in `pyproject.toml`; and 27 pre-existing `ruff` errors in those same FYERS files. **Left untouched on purpose** - the lab polish must not silently absorb unrelated broker work. A full `make check` stays red until those are resolved on their own branch.
10. **Open, deliberately NOT done (features, not polish):** no search/filter on the section 01 research-object library (none at any count - a real gap at 50+ strategies); no line numbers or syntax highlighting in the section 05 editor; both grids still shrink columns indefinitely rather than scrolling at a minimum table width (elide now makes the truncation honest, but a horizontal scroll is the real fix); `LabHeader` still declares ~10 `in property` fields the screen never reads (`params`, `matrix`, `board-*`, `equity-buy/sell`, `drawdown-buy/sell`, `compare-ranking`, `filter-active`) and 13 callbacks with no call site - removing them is a Rust refactor across `lab.rs` / `shell.rs` / `vayren-strategy-lab-view`, not a polish pass.

**Previous update (fyers-websocket-integration-phase2, 2026-10-06):** VAYREN PHASE 2 — FYERS REAL-TIME MARKET + ORDER WEBSOCKET INTEGRATION COMPLETED & VERIFIED.
- **Scope & Isolation:** Phase 2 Only. Live market data WebSocket (`FyersLiveMarketData`) and real-time order/trade WebSocket streaming (`FyersSessionAdapter`). No UI redesign, no OBR C1C4 modifications, no risk formula modifications, no EC2.
- **FYERS Real-Time Market Data WebSocket (`FyersLiveMarketData`):**
  - Implements `MarketDataFace` and `MarketDataProvider` in `src/broker/providers/fyers/live_market_data.py`.
  - Connects via `FyersDataSocket`, subscribes to NSE symbols with auto-resubscription upon reconnection.
  - Normalizes FYERS tick payloads into VAYREN's broker-neutral `MarketEvent` pipeline (`TradeEvent`, `QuoteEvent`, `CandleEvent`).
  - Preserves symbol, LTP, volume, timestamp (ISO-8601 UTC), bid/ask with quantities, and OHLC.
  - Exposes thread-safe queue drained non-blockingly via `poll()`.
  - Reports discrete connection states (`DISCONNECTED`, `CONNECTING`, `CONNECTED`, `RECONNECTING`, `ERROR`) and `health()`.
- **FYERS Real-Time Order & Trade WebSocket (`FyersSessionAdapter`):**
  - Integrated `FyersOrderSocket` in `src/broker/providers/fyers/session_adapter.py` streaming `orders` and `trades`.
  - Emits normalized VAYREN events (`ack`, `fill`, `reject`, `cancel`) for order lifecycle progression.
  - Accurate partial fill handling: position ledger updates strictly on confirmed filled quantity, never requested quantity.
  - Deduplication: Maintains `_seen_fills` preventing duplicate fills across socket retransmissions.
  - Fallback: Gracefully drains WebSocket event queue first, falling back to REST poll if socket is idle/disconnected.
  - Dedicated health tracking: `order_stream_health()`.
- **Safety Gates & Isolation:**
  - `LiveSession._submit`: Hard gate blocks live order dispatch if broker reports disconnected/unhealthy.
  - `PAPER` and `SANDBOX` modes remain strictly isolated and never hit live WebSockets or broker endpoints.
- **Verification:**
  - 11/11 tests passing in `src/broker/tests/test_fyers_websocket.py`.
  - 192/192 broker tests passing in `src/broker/tests/`.
  - 12/12 execution tests passing in `src/execution/tests/`.
  - `python tools/validate_language_ownership.py`: PASSED (325 files checked).
  - `cargo check --workspace`: PASSED cleanly.
  - Zero git push performed (local only per non-negotiable rules).

**Previous update (fyers-live-execution-phase1, 2026-10-06):** VAYREN PHASE 1 — FYERS LIVE ORDER EXECUTION ADAPTER COMPLETED & VERIFIED.
- **Scope & Isolation:** Phase 1 Only. Extended existing `FyersSessionAdapter` in `src/broker/providers/fyers/session_adapter.py`. No UI redesign, no OBR C1C4 modifications, no risk formula modifications, no WebSockets (Phase 2), no EC2.
- **FYERS API v3 Order Execution Implemented:**
  - `place_order(plan, client_order_id, idempotency_key=None) -> str`: POST `/api/v3/orders/sync`. Translates VAYREN order models into FYERS request format (`symbol`, `qty`, `type`, `side`, `productType`, `limitPrice`, `stopPrice`, `validity`, `disclosedQty`, `offlineOrder`, `orderTag`).
  - `cancel_order(broker_order_id) -> bool`: DELETE `/api/v3/orders/sync`.
  - `modify_order(broker_order_id, quantity, price) -> bool`: PUT `/api/v3/orders/sync`.
  - `stream_events() -> tuple[dict, ...]`: Polls FYERS orderbook `/api/v3/orders` (`orderBook`), diffs order state and emits fills, rejects, cancels.
  - Safe structured logging without logging credentials (`ORDER_REQUEST`, `ORDER_RESPONSE`, `ORDER_REJECTED`).
- **Response & Error Handling:** Real broker order ID extracted from response `id`. Failures remain failures (never fabricated as success). Normalized error mapping for auth failure, insufficient funds, invalid symbol, market closed, network timeouts.
- **Mode Separation & Safety:** Adapter environment pinned to `LIVE`. Fails closed if not connected or credentials missing. `PAPER` and `SANDBOX` remain isolated and cannot call the FYERS LIVE endpoint.

**Previous update (live-ui-clarity-pass-phase2, 2026-10-05):** LIVE UI CLARITY PASS PHASE 2
1. **ROOT CAUSE FOUND \u2014 the fake risk numbers were BACKEND-side, not UI-side.** `live_trading_service._watchlist` hardcoded demo reference levels for three symbols (KAYNES entry 1218.50 / stop 1245.00, RVNL 451.20/462.30, POWERGRID 308.90/316.20) and, for EVERY other symbol, invented `ref_high = ltp * 1.006` and `ref_low = ltp * 0.986`. Those made-up levels were then fed into the REAL `CapitalRiskEngine.compute_qty`, which is why the screen showed a confident `Risk/share \u20b926.50 / Max \u20b9600 / Qty 22 / Planned \u20b9583 / 97.2%` for a stock whose C1-C4 candles the backend had never computed. **A risk figure derived from an invented level is worse than no figure** \u2014 it looks validated and it is not. All three branches are deleted; `entry_price` now comes only from a real open position's ledger `avg_price`, `stop_price`/ref levels stay `None` until the session reports them, and `compute_qty` is called ONLY when both entry and stop are real. Consequence to expect: the risk columns read `\u2014` for most rows until real levels flow. That is the honest state, not a regression.
2. **The order pipeline is now the ENGINE's own state machine (STEPS 12/13).** Root cause: `ExecutionEngine::snapshot()` already emitted `OrderSnapshot::reason` (the real rejection text) and `OrderSnapshot::history` (the real legal transition sequence), but `live_trading_service.snapshot()` forwarded neither \u2014 so the UI had to paint a decorative `SIGNAL \u2192 RISK OK \u2192 SENT \u2192 ACK \u2192 WORKING \u2192 FILLED` strip in solid green with no backend behind it, which is exactly what STEP 12 forbids. Both fields are now forwarded. `OrderRow` gained `reason`, `filled_qty`, `history`; `live::order_pipeline()` walks the real `order_state` vocabulary (CREATED/VALIDATED/SUBMITTED/ACKNOWLEDGED/FILLED/REJECTED/CANCELLED/EXPIRED) and marks a step `reached` only when the order's history contains it. With no backend order the pipeline is EMPTY and the screen prints `NO ORDER \u2014 backend has not created one for this symbol`. `StockDetailView.order_reason` shows the engine's rejection text verbatim (or the risk verdict that stopped creation). **A real bug the new tests caught in my own first implementation:** every reached step was marked `current`; now exactly one step is live \u2014 the furthest the engine got \u2014 and a failure is terminal, never `current`.
3. **SIGNAL / ORDER / POSITION are now three independent fields (STEPS 11/27).** `StockDetailView` gained `signal` and `order`; previously only POSITION had a field, so the inspector could not answer "is there a signal?" or "what happened to my order?" without cross-reading the watchlist row. `NONE` is the honest value when the backend has nothing, and each carries its own tone (SELL=red, FILLED=green, WORKING=amber, NONE=grey).
4. **Nine disjoint filter buckets (STEP 26).** The chip set is now `ALL / READY / SIGNAL / ORDER / IN POSITION / WAITING / NO DATA / RISK BLOCKED / REJECTED`, and each chip's COUNT and each chip's ROW FILTER are literally the same predicate closure \u2014 a chip cannot claim N and reveal a different number of rows. They were made DISJOINT after the STEP 37 probe showed a row counted in both `WAITING` and `RISK BLOCKED`: `RISK BLOCKED` is reserved for rows that HAVE entry/stop and were refused by the engine, `WAITING` for rows with no levels and nothing actionable. `READY` now also excludes rows with a live order. `LiveState::risk_engine_agrees()` is the single capital+levels verdict both counts share.
5. **STEP 29 \u2014 current open risk vs planned risk, honestly.** `StockDetailView.current_open_risk: Option<f64>` is computed ONLY from a real open position's quantity, entry and stop (`qty * abs(stop - entry)`). With no stop the panel renders `\u2014`; it never derives a risk distance from LTP. The inspector shows PLANNED RISK and CURRENT OPEN RISK on separate rows because they are different numbers for different moments.
6. **STEP 30 \u2014 event stream made scannable, not invented.** `_describe_entry` still reads the journal payload exclusively, but the wording is now an explicit operational verb: `SELL SIGNAL GENERATED \u00b7 <id>`, `ORDER SUBMITTED \u00b7 <id>`, `ORDER ACKNOWLEDGED BY BROKER \u00b7 <id>`, `FILLED \u00b7 <qty> @ <px>`, `REJECTED \u00b7 <id>: <engine reason>`, `RISK BLOCKED \u00b7 <reasons>`, `ORDER NOT SENT \u00b7 live not armed`, `STALE DATA \u00b7 seq <n>`, `STRATEGY ERROR \u00b7 <reason>`. Same real data, readable as a sequence of things that happened.
7. **STEP 36 \u2014 the 20-state visual matrix now EXISTS as a test.** `live_state_matrix_renders_at_the_primary_target_viewport` renders all twenty acceptance states offscreen at 1920x1080 (3 mode/status combos, 4 WS/MD combos, 4 order states, 3 position sides, 3 risk readiness, 2 reconciliation, 60-symbol scale) and holds each to the same three health floors as the idle matrix \u2014 panels paint, the identity accent renders, glyphs rasterize, no frame is a void. A state that crashes or renders unreadable fails here.
8. **STEP 37 \u2014 verified by reading the real projection, not by assertion.** A temporary probe (`step37_probe.rs`, since removed) projected two states and printed the eleven answers. LIVE + STOPPED: `strategy=OBR C1C4, mode=LIVE, status=\u25cf STOPPED, ws=CONNECTED 38 ms, md=STREAMING 0.4s, order=NONE, signal=NONE, position=FLAT, qty=\u2014, risk/share=26.50, max risk=\u2014, blockers=` the three backend reasons verbatim. LIVE + RUNNING: `status=\u25cf RUNNING, order=ACKNOWLEDGED, qty=22, max risk=\u20b9600.00, planned=\u20b9583.00, util=97.2%, blockers=(none), risk=READY 4x LEV \u00b7 \u20b9600 MAX RISK/STOCK`. **All eleven answers are reachable without searching the screen**, and the word ACTIVE never labels execution state. Note `risk/share` is the only risk figure that survives without capital \u2014 correct, since price distance is independent of broker funds.
9. **Verification (all real, this phase):** `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib` 236/236; `--lib live` 43/43 (9 new pipeline tests); `cargo test -p vayren-shell --lib` 53/53; `--test live_render_snapshot` 2/2 (idle matrix + the new 20-state matrix); `cargo test --workspace --exclude vayren-shell` all targets green (core 403, domain 236, views 36); `python -m pytest src -q` 383/383; `pyright` 0 errors; `ruff check` + `format --check` clean; `cargo fmt --all -- --check` clean; all 8 validators PASS (repo_graph rebuilt to 4630 entities, language_ownership 322 files); `cargo build -p vayren-shell` succeeds (the debug exe rebuilt once the app was closed).
10. **Known pre-existing / not mine:** `Cargo.toml` (`[profile.dev] debug = 0`) and `tools/validate_scope.py` (domain routing) are modified in this tree but were NOT changed by this pass. `docs/ai_memory.md` still carries historical 3-layer cp437 mojibake (`\u0393\u00c7\u00f6` for em-dash) from old PowerShell round-trips in the older entries; the header and this entry are clean. No UI string is affected.
11. **Open (needs a backend change, deliberately NOT faked here):** the live session never exposes the strategy's C1-C4 reference window / break level / stop to this layer (`SIGNAL_GENERATED` carries only `strategy_id`, `signal_id`, `event_seq`), so per-stock `ENTRY`, `STOP`, `REF HIGH/LOW` and therefore `QTY`/`PLANNED RISK` stay `\u2014` while the strategy is idle. Surfacing them means adding the levels to the `SIGNAL_GENERATED` journal payload \u2014 a real backend change, out of scope for a UI clarity pass.


**Latest update (live-ui-clarity-pass, 2026-10-05):** LIVE UI STATE-CLARITY PASS \u2014 no-feature, presentation-only (local only, no commit/push). Files: `crates/vayren-domain/src/live.rs`, `crates/vayren-shell/ui/live.slint`, `crates/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs`, `src/app/headless.py`, `src/risk/models.py`. **Zero trading logic touched** (OBR C1C4, CapitalRiskEngine, risk validation, broker routing, SL, EOD, reconciliation all untouched).
1. **Text encoding (STEP 1) \u2014 the source files were never the bug.** Every UI source (`live.slint`, `live.rs`, `shell.rs`, `palette.slint`) was already valid UTF-8 with correct `U+2014 \u2014 / U+25CF \u25CF / U+2713 \u2713 / U+20B9 \u20b9`. The real gap was the PIPE: `_emit()` used bare `json.dumps(...)` and `_progress_emitter()` wrote `json.dumps(...)` without pinning encoding, so any non-UTF-8 stdout config would transcode `\u20b9` to `?` before the Rust bridge ever saw it. Both now call `stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")` and pass `ensure_ascii=False`. **Verified byte-exact round-trip:** `\u20b9583 \u00b7 \u2713 \u25CF \u2014 \u2192 \u00d7` arrives unchanged. WebSocket transport logic itself is UNCHANGED.
2. **`ACTIVE` ambiguity removed (STEPS 2/3/23) \u2014 root cause was a REGISTRY word, not a session fact.** `project()` derived the strategy card status from `strategy_facts.status`, which is the strategy-registry's own word for "loaded/armed" (`ACTIVE`). A configured-but-stopped session therefore printed `ACTIVE \u00b7 30m` under the strategy name while the footer said STOPPED \u2014 the exact contradiction reported. Now `strategy_sub` carries only descriptive facts (`timeframe \u00b7 direction`), and the card/session status comes solely from `state.session.label()` (the backend lifecycle), with `kill_halted` outranking it. The word `ACTIVE` no longer labels execution state anywhere in the LIVE surface. The card title `ACTIVE STRATEGY` became `STRATEGY`, and the duplicated `LIVE STATUS` headline next to a contradictory badge was removed (STEP 23).
3. **Fabricated numbers deleted (STEP 34's "no fake state").** `ActiveStrategyView` shipped hardcoded `started_at: "09:15:00 IST"`, `today_signals: "2 generated (2 Short)"`, a `strategy_logic` sentence describing C1-C4 mechanics that no backend sent, and `symbols_count: "{n} NSE Equity"`. All four now derive from real snapshot facts (`snapshot_time`, counted non-empty signals, `pnl.orders`, `direction`/`reference_window`/`timeframe`), and render `\u2014` when unreported. Same for the Slint-side fallbacks that invented `"52 ACTIVE"`, `"2 OPEN"`, `"09:15:00"`, `"OBR C1C4 (RUNNING)"`, `"42 ms"`, `"0.4s"`, `"52 symbols"` \u2014 all now honest `\u2014` / `format!("{:.0} ms", ...)` / real counts.
4. **MODE and STATUS are now independent and both dominant (STEPS 3/4/17).** The LiveTopCard mode badge tone moved from `1` (green) to `3` (neg) for LIVE, because a red LIVE chip is a risk posture, not an all-clear. `LiveScreen.start-verdict` is a single derived string: `\u25cf RUNNING` (stoppable session) / `\u2715 START BLOCKED` (backend reported blockers) / `\u2713 READY TO START` \u2014 and it is NEVER set by a click, so no optimistic state is reachable.
5. **Start blockers consolidated into ONE block (STEP 5).** Blockers were rendered in the readiness card, as a `VBanner`, and again implicitly via the footer verdict. Now a single `LIVE READINESS` card owns a 30px verdict bar plus a `WHY START IS DISABLED` bullet list \u2014 each backend reason exactly once \u2014 and the footer states only the verdict, never the reasons. An empty blocker list prints `No blockers reported by the backend.` rather than silently reading as safe.
6. **Risk card names WHY it is not ready (STEP 6).** `NOT READY` \u2192 `\u25cf NOT READY`, and the sub-line now reports the missing fact by name (`BROKER CAPITAL: NOT AVAILABLE`, or the actual capital source when it is not `broker`) instead of the ambiguous `CAPITAL: NOT AVAILABLE`. With capital present the sub-line reads `4x LEV \u00b7 \u20b9600 MAX RISK/STOCK` (was `MAX`), so the ceiling's UNIT is unambiguous. Sizing numbers (QTY / PLANNED / UTIL) still fail closed to `\u2014` when capital is unavailable.
7. **Per-stock risk columns added (STEPS 9/28).** `WatchlistRowView` gained `max_allowed_risk`, `risk_util`, `risk_util_tone`. `max_allowed_risk` is the engine's own `max_allowed_risk()` per row (never one global number reused), and `risk_util_tone` escalates 0 \u2192 2 at 80% \u2192 3 at 95% so a row states its own pressure. `CapitalRiskEngine::compute_qty`/`validate_planned_risk` were NOT modified \u2014 the UI reads them, it does not reimplement them (STEP 7/34).
8. **Inspector clipping at 150% scale fixed (STEP 24) \u2014 root cause was a dead tier function.** `LiveTiers.inspector-width()` existed but `LiveScreen.inspector-w` was hardcoded `340px`, and the panel sets `clip: true`, so the fixed label columns (90 + 50 + 70 px plus gaps) were cut off-frame on the right. `inspector-w` is now bound to the tier function (`max(440px, min(520px, w * 0.34))`), `inspector-min-width()` raised to 440px, and both pinned shell assertions updated (520 capped / 440 floor).
9. **Watchlist no longer overflows its panel (STEP 25).** All 17 columns were fixed-width (~1200px), guaranteeing horizontal overflow at 150%. The four reference-candle columns (BREAK C1/C4, TRIGGER, STOP LOSS) plus MAX RISK / UTIL % / P&L now sit behind `watchlist-columns-wide` (`root.width >= 1120px`), derived from the table's OWN width so it collapses before it can clip. The operational spine (SYMBOL, LTP, SIGNAL, ORDER, POSITION, QTY, RISK/SH, PLANNED, STATUS) is always present, and row capacity stays runtime-driven (no hardcoded 22/52).
10. **Verification (all real):** `cargo check -p vayren-domain` PASS; `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib live` 36/36 PASS; `cargo check -p vayren-shell` PASS (only preexisting unused-var warnings); `cargo test -p vayren-shell --lib` 53/53 PASS; `cargo test -p vayren-shell --test live_render_snapshot` 1/1 PASS across all viewport tiers; `pytest src/app/tests/test_live_actions.py` 8/8 PASS; `python tools/validate_language_ownership.py` PASS (322 files); `python tools/repo_graph.py --build` + `validate_repo_graph.py` PASS (4622 entities, 7865 relationships, 0 unresolved \u2014 the rebuild was REQUIRED because the Slint line shifts broke the `validators` CI job previously).
11. **Known pre-existing, unrelated:** `perf_trades::bench_trade_search_and_filter_stay_bounded` is a timing-based bench that failed once under parallel test load and passed on re-run both with and without these changes (proven by a `git stash` A/B). `docs/ai_memory.md` itself carries historical 3-layer `cp437` mojibake (`\u0393\u00c7\u00f6` for `\u2014`) introduced by old PowerShell text round-trips; it is documentation-only, was left as-is rather than rewrite git history, and no UI string is affected.

**Previous update (live-risk-engine-source-of-truth, 2026-10-04):** VAYREN CAPITAL RISK ENGINE AUTHORITATIVE SEMANTICS & UI FAIL-CLOSED SIZING COMPLETED & VERIFIED.
1. **CapitalRiskEngine Authoritative Semantics (`crates/vayren-domain/src/live.rs`):**
   - Price risk is completely decoupled from broker capital: `risk_per_share = abs(stop_price - entry_price)`. It is displayed for every stock whenever entry and stop prices exist.
   - Broker capital determines `raw_capital`, `effective_capital`, `max_allowed_risk`.
   - Capital + price risk determine `calculated_qty` and `planned_risk`.
   - Enforced fail-closed behavior: `has_valid_capital(&self) -> bool` strictly checks positive broker capital in LIVE mode and valid capital sources in paper/sandbox modes.
   - If broker capital is unavailable or 0: displays `CAPITAL: NOT AVAILABLE`, `MAX RISK: —`, `QTY: —`, `PLANNED RISK: —`, and `RISK STATUS: NOT READY` (badge tone 3).
   - Removed all hardcoded sample capital fallbacks (₹600 / 22 / ₹583).
   - Added explicit label `CAPITAL SOURCE: BROKER` (or matching capital source).
2. **Native Slint UI Separation (`crates/vayren-shell/ui/live.slint`):**
   - Top Card 5 (RISK ENGINE): dynamically binds `root.bar.risk-label` and `root.bar.risk-sub` (e.g. `4x LEV · ₹600 MAX` or `CAPITAL: NOT AVAILABLE` when blocked).
   - Selected Stock Details Inspector: clearly separates 3 distinct sections:
     a) `CAPITAL & RISK LIMITS`: `CAPITAL SOURCE: BROKER` badge, Broker Capital, Effective Capital, Max Risk / Trade.
     b) `REFERENCE LEVELS & PRICE RISK (C1-C4)`: Ref High, Ref Low, Trigger/Entry, Stop Loss, Risk / Share.
     c) `POSITION & RISK SIZING`: Computed Qty, Planned Risk, Risk Util, and dynamic validation banner (`[✓] RISK VALIDATED` or `[✕] RISK STATUS: NOT READY · Capital not available`).
3. **Bridge & Host Windows Updates:**
   - Synchronized `crates/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs` and `crates/vayren-live-view/src/lib.rs` with `risk_sub`, `broker_capital`, `effective_capital`, `max_allowed_risk`, and `capital_source`.

**Previous update (stock-backtest-failure-fixed, 2026-10-04):** STOCK BACKTEST MULTI-SYMBOL CRASH & WATCHDOG FIXED (local only, no commit/push).
1. **Raw Bar Clamping / Sanitization (`src/app/services/market_data_service.py`):**
   - Root cause: Real-world market feeds (Zerodha SQLite) have occasional bad ticks where `open < low` or `close > high` (e.g. `ASTRAMICRO` on 2024-06-25 had `open=963.0` while `low=966.1`), causing `Bar.__post_init__` to raise `ValueError: open (963.0) must lie in [low, high]` and aborting the entire 526-symbol backtest pass.
   - Fix: Enforced `fh = max(fh, fo, fc)` and `fl = min(fl, fo, fc)` in `_read_rows` and `_bucket_bar` so high and low always envelop open and close, preventing validation errors from unaligned raw feed records.
2. **Resilient Symbol Loop Across Universe (`src/app/services/backtest_service.py`):**
   - In `_prefetch_bars._load`: Caught `(MarketDataError, Exception)` so an unreadable symbol or data error does not kill the thread pool.
   - In `run_backtest`: Instead of aborting the entire 526-symbol universe when one symbol fails data loading or execution, record `run.symbol_failed(symbol)` (incrementing the Failed counter) and continue to remaining symbols in deterministic order. Fails closed only if zero symbols produce results (`if not ranking:`).
3. **Watchdog Elapsed Time Glitch (`src/app/progress.py`):**
   - In `load_progress`: Set `self._symbol_started = time.monotonic()` so `current_elapsed` reflects actual symbol load duration rather than host system uptime (~97 hours), fixing the false `LONG-RUNNING — 97:01:19` watchdog warning.
4. **UI Running Badge State (`crates/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs`):**
   - In `apply_lab_progress`: Set `header.state_label = view.state_label.into()` and `header.state_tone = view.state_tone` so the state label dynamically updates to `● RUNNING…` instead of remaining stuck on a stale `✕ FAILED`.
5. **Verification:**
   - Ran `run_backtest_repro.py` on all 526 symbols with `OBR` strategy: all 526 symbols completed, producing 72,706 trades and 526 ranked stocks with status `complete`.
   - `cargo check -p vayren-shell` & `cargo build -p vayren-shell`: PASSED.
   - `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib`: 226/226 PASSED.
   - `pytest src/app/tests src/market/tests -q`: 111/111 PASSED.
   - `python tools/validate_language_ownership.py`: 322/322 files PASSED.
   - `ruff check`: All checks passed.

**Previous update (strategy-lab-bugs-and-perf-fixed, 2026-10-04):** STRATEGY LAB END-TO-END AUDIT, BUG FIXES & 2x SPEEDUP (local only, no commit/push).
1. **Performance & Data Loading (Python, ~2x speedup):**
   - Profiled via `cProfile` on live 30m intraday data: `get_bars` time dropped from 4.53s to 2.36s.
   - `src/market/models/bar.py`: Replaced slow ABC `isinstance(value, numbers.Real)` with C-level `isinstance(value, (int, float))` in `_is_number`. Added `slots=True` to `Bar` and `BarView` (`src/strategy/runtime.py`).
   - `src/app/services/market_data_service.py`: Added `_base_seconds_cache` avoiding redundant SQLite reads on every symbol; fixed millisecond timestamp parsing (`len(text) >= 19`); optimized OHLCV verification and vector aggregation loop.
2. **Search Filter & Ranking Table Fix (Rust Domain):**
   - `crates/vayren-domain/src/lab.rs`: Added `order: Vec<u32>` caching to `VirtualWindow`. Fixed false-empty search bug in Trade Blotter (`rebuild_trade_view`) and Ranking Grid (`VirtualWindow::rebuild`). Search changes (e.g. "INFY" -> "TCS") and filter clear now correctly filter the cached sorted order without losing sort or dropping matching rows. Added regression unit test `search_filter_transitions_preserve_sort_and_find_results`.
3. **UI Model Thrashing Elimination (Rust Shell):**
   - `crates/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs` & `src/main.rs`: Created `apply_lab_progress` for 50ms progress ticks that only updates scalar properties on `LabHeader` without recreating or allocating 12+ Slint `VecModel`s (library, kpis, date presets, ranking, blotter, equity, drawdown, etc.), eliminating UI stalls during backtests.
4. **Equity Curve & Drawdown View (Slint + Shell):**
   - `crates/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs`: Uniformly downsamples curves with >300 points across the complete series timeline in `to_points` instead of truncating at 300 points.
   - `crates/vayren-shell/ui/lab.slint`: Changed equity curve point color to profit/loss tone (`LabPalette.green` for profit, `LabPalette.lab-red` for loss) and updated subtitle to "sampled across N points".
5. **Strategy Save Pipeline Wired (Shell + Headless):**
   - Added `SaveLabStrategy` to `BackendCommand` (`crates/vayren-shell/src/python_bridge.rs`).
   - Wired `on_lab_save_requested` via `fetch_lab_save` in `crates/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs` and `main.rs`.
   - Handled `save_lab_strategy` in `src/app/headless.py` writing code to `strategy_dir` and clearing metadata caches.
6. **Verification:**
   - `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib`: 226/226 PASSED in 0.15s.
   - `cargo test -p vayren-shell --lib`: 53/53 PASSED in 20.43s.

**Previous update (lab-ux-10, 2026-10-04):** STRATEGY LAB UX -> 10/10 - no-feature, Slint-only (local only, no commit/push). Files: `crates/vayren-shell/ui/lab.slint`, `crates/vayren-shell/ui/palette.slint`. Zero Rust and zero Python lines changed. Score 6.2/10 -> ~9/10.
1. **Two dead dropdown affordances, found by pixel probe (not by reading).** `LabCtlButton` packs `[label, caret]` in a `HorizontalLayout` that, inside a bare `Rectangle` in Slint 1.17, sizes to its CONTENT instead of the button width - so `horizontal-stretch: 1` had no slack to distribute and the caret rendered flush against the label's last glyph ("30m|Y"). `start`, `center` and a stretch factor all produced the same packed block. Replaced with absolute geometry (`x: 11px` / `width: parent.width - 11px - 15px - 11px`, caret at `parent.width - 11px - 9px`). Separately, a glyph probe across the resolved UI font found `U+25BE "▾"` paints NOTHING (every ▾ control in the lab - UNIVERSE, TIMEFRAME, Sort by, the Equity view selector - was a static-looking word) while `↓ → ∨ ✓ ● ˅ ▼ ⊘ ⏎` all paint. Caret is now `▼` (U+25BC). Probe method worth reusing: put candidate codepoints in one always-rendered `Text`, render, then read lit-pixel columns per control.
2. **Contrast floor fixed at the token level.** `LabPalette.dim` was `#525e67` (2.79:1 on `field-bg`) and `lab-faint` `#39434b` (1.83:1) - both failing WCAG AA 4.5:1, and they carried EVERY eyebrow, table header, placeholder, meta label and em-dash. Both are now `#75818b` (4.65:1 on `field-bg`, 5.01:1 on `lab-bg`). They are deliberately the SAME value: hierarchy now comes from size and weight, not from a contrast step nobody can read. Micro type floor raised 8.5/9/9.5px -> 10/10.5px across `lab.slint` (only `LabPalette.*` consumers; no other surface reads these tokens).
3. **Unsized-child-absorbs-slack, three sites.** A `VerticalLayout` child with no stated height takes ALL leftover column space, which is what pushed the §01 MODE segmented ~130px below the description (`alignment: end` then pinned it to the bottom of the absorbed row, centred in a column owning no other control). Fixed with explicit heights + one trailing `vertical-stretch: 1` slack sink in §01, and an explicit height on the §05 "Strategy source" caption row that had been floating in a 280px void. **When fixing one of these, check the others: stating the mode row's height alone just moved the slack into the strategy-name Text.**
4. **Painted lies removed.** The §02 `LabFieldLabel` "EDIT" hint on UNIVERSE / TIMEFRAME / CAPITAL was a `TouchArea { enabled: false }` - it LOOKED like a link and did nothing (only DATE RANGE's `CUSTOM` was real, via `hint-accent`). Removed all three; the file's own rule at the component ("the affordance has to be real, not painted") was being violated by its own call sites.
5. **Redundancy removed by subtraction, no new content.** UNIVERSE chip row now renders only when `chips-more-line != ""` (i.e. only past `UNIVERSE_CHIP_CAP`, where it carries symbols the button line has stopped naming) - it used to print "A, B, C" verbatim 15px under a button already saying "A, B, C". The §05 primary button no longer becomes a filled pill reading "Saved"; it always reads "Save changes" and uses `btn-idle-bg` when clean, with the STATE owned by the one status line beside it (which now tints amber when dirty). `CTRL + Y` is plain text again, not an 18px bordered pill indistinguishable from a state chip. The footer row collapses to nothing when `progress-label` is empty (was a fixed 56px + 24px tail holding one optional string). The EQUITY header no longer prints "cumulative P&L ·" next to a selector already labelled "Cumulative P&L".
6. **Grouping/alignment fixes.** §03 KPI cells were `alignment: center`, packing `[label, value]` into a block so "TRADES" and its value touched and read as the token "TRADES-"; now absolutely placed label-left / value-right with the SCALE/QUALITY/RISK headers pinned to the same left edge (`horizontal-alignment` is inert on an unconstrained `Text` - it needs `x` AND `width`). The §03 receipt flag is left-aligned on the same 18px edge as the audit lines below instead of floating alone in a 46px centred band. `LabCtlInput` and the DATE RANGE FROM/TO halves moved off `alignment: center` so amounts and dates start on their field's own padding edge.
7. **Lens exclusivity.** EQUITY rendered under the Performance lens AND as its own lens-2 page, so the lens bar offered a tab for a panel the default tab already showed further down the page. Now lens-2 only, matching how Trades has always behaved.
8. **Deliberately NOT done (would be new features, out of scope):** no search/filter on the §01 strategy library (it has none at any count - a real gap at 50+ strategies), no line numbers or syntax highlighting in the §05 editor, no `MIN`/`Est. runtime`-style validation copy. A `MORE Y` caret in the TIMEFRAME segmented was attempted and REVERTED - at ~41px the caret clipped the word to "M...", and putting it in a sub-layout handed the cell a wider max-width so `horizontal-stretch` broke the equal-cell invariant the component exists to protect. Kept the documented original.
9. **Verification.** `cargo test -p vayren-shell` 68/68 PASS (incl. the three lab pixel regressions `lab_config_render` 7/7, `lab_scroll`, `lab_select` - the shared 19/36/30 control baseline survived every edit). `cargo test --workspace --exclude vayren-shell` 672/672 PASS. `validate_language_ownership` 322 files, `validate_imports`, `validate_authority` (19 slint), `validate_repo_graph` 4602 entities / 0 unresolved, `validate_routes`, `validate_structure`, `validate_architecture_gate` - all PASS. Known pre-existing, unrelated: `cargo test -p vayren-shell --doc` fails with colliding `StableCrateId` for `vayren_domain` (duplicate crate in the workspace, from in-flight broker-connection work). Stale Slint codegen also bit once - a `timer0 on Pin<&InnerComponent_rectangle_977>` rustc error from the generated `app.rs`; `cargo clean -p vayren-shell` cleared it, no code change.

Files: `crates/vayren-shell/ui/broker_connection.slint` (+`app.slint`, `crates/vayren-system-view/ui/system_host.slint` pass-throughs), `crates/vayren-domain/src/broker_connection.rs`, `crates/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs`, `crates/vayren-system-view/src/lib.rs`.
1. Gap closed: the backend already sends `saved` per credential field and Rust pre-filled saved fields with the `SAVED_CREDENTIAL_SENTINEL`, but the UI never RENDERED the fact. Added a `SAVED` chip beside each field (54x18, pos-dim) plus a new `saved-sentinel` property, so the chip shows only while the field still holds the untouched sentinel and disappears the moment the user retypes - it can never claim a stale save. `SAVED_CREDENTIAL_SENTINEL` is now a public const in `shell.rs` (prefill and the UI comparison share one value).
2. Interaction polish: hover + press + animated (120ms ease-out) on broker rows, Add Broker, PASTE, SHOW/HIDE, COPY/REMOVE and the help card; `mouse-cursor: pointer` everywhere; focused inputs lift to `bg2` with an accent border, brand-tinted text selection, and Enter/Ctrl+Enter submit + Escape cancel routed through ONE `submit()` function so the keyboard can never fire a different action than the button; timer-driven "COPIED" receipt; the active progress step pulses only while the backend reports an authentication round-trip (`pulse-timer.running = busy()`).
3. Destructive action is now arm-then-confirm: REMOVE BROKER reveals an inline "YES, REMOVE / KEEP" pair stating exactly what is deleted (saved credentials) and what is not (orders, positions, history). Escape disarms.
4. Semantic color fix: an unconnected venue was painted RED (`pill_tone`/`status_tone` = 3) even though nothing had failed. Not-configured and ready are now MUTED (0); red is reserved for `Failed` alone, and the pill surface follows the tone via `pill-surface()`. Sidebar rows follow the same rule.
5. State-aware copy + layout balance: added `BrokerWorkspace::description()` so a live session stops reading "Connect your FYERS account to start trading." (now "Connected: <reason>"); the two-column row stretches so the vault footnote (left) and COPY/REMOVE + help (right) land on the card's bottom edge instead of leaving ~280px of trailing dead space; DISCONNECT BROKER is capped at 220px so a destructive action never reads as a full-width primary control; `disconnect-visible` (previously bound but unused) now gates that button; venue subtitle falls back to `-`; honest `VEmptyState` when a venue reports no credential schema.
6. Verification: `cargo test -p vayren-domain --lib` 225/225 PASS, `cargo test -p vayren-shell --lib` 53/53 PASS, `cargo test -p vayren-system-view` 8/8 PASS, clippy clean on touched code, `validate_authority/structure/imports/language_ownership/routes/repo_graph/architecture_gate` all PASS. `docs/repo_graph.json` REBUILT (4602 entities) - Slint line numbers moved, which is exactly what broke the `validators` CI job before. Visual evidence via `vayren-system-view/tests/shot.rs` (offscreen 1440x900 and 1440x620 renders, fixture restored afterwards).

**Previous update (zoom-anchored-on-cursor, 2026-10-02):** MARKET CHART — ZOOM IS CURSOR-ANCHORED (TradingView parity), merged to main via fix/chart-zoom-cursor-anchor.
1. **Root cause (one line, `vayren-domain/src/market.rs` `MarketAction::WheelZoom`):** the anchor was `(...).clamp(self.first, total - 1)`. A cursor in the empty right-hand future area (the anchored view always leaves `RIGHT_MARGIN_FRACTION` = 15% empty) therefore re-anchored every wheel notch to the NEWEST BAR, so the candles slid sideways under a stationary mouse. Anchoring on data was already right; the clamp only bit past the last bar.
2. **Fix:** `anchor_bar = self.first + fraction * count as f64` — no data clamp. The viewport offset is still clamped (`max(0.0)` + `clamp_first`), so the data edges still bound the view; the invariant `new_first + fraction*new_count == old_first + fraction*old_count` makes the cursor point exactly stationary in both directions, and repeated in/out round trips restore `first`/`count` bit-for-bit.
3. **Wheel direction fact (measured through the real ABI, do not "fix" it again):** `market.slint` `scroll-event` sends `-steps`, so `delta-y > 0` zooms IN, `delta-y < 0` zooms OUT, one step = 120 logical px. The anchor comes from the TRACKED pointer (`self.mouse-x / plot.width`), not the scroll position — a bare scroll event with no prior pointer move anchors on the stale mouse position. The future area sits at window-x ≈ 0.84…0.95 (plot fraction ≈ 0.85…1.0 at 1280x720); beyond the plot the scroll event is not consumed by the chart.
4. **Untouched by design:** `market.slint`, `x_of`/`index_at`/`visible_window`, pan, price zoom, candle data/rendering, indicators/overlays, timeframe.
5. **Verification:** domain acceptance test `zoom_is_anchored_on_the_cursor_not_the_chart` (A middle candle centre, B left candle repeated 6x, C right candle centre, D future area at fx 0.70/0.85/0.95/1.0, E 10 in/out round trips) + `zoom_direction_and_scale_contract_hold`. Pixel test `render_market.rs::zoom_round_trip_at_the_cursor_restores_the_frame` — mid-chart AND future-area cursor, zoom in then out at the same point, byte-identical frame; confirmed it FAILS on the pre-fix clamp and passes after. Branch CI: validators/quality/test-shard-pure PASS; `cargo test --workspace` locally = 723 passed / 0 failed; `cargo fmt --check` clean.
6. **Build-loop fact (still true):** a RUNNING `target/debug/vayren-shell.exe` makes `cargo build`/`cargo test` fail with `failed to remove file … vayren-shell.exe`. Close the app before rebuilding.

**Previous update (crosshair-viewport-bound, 2026-10-02):** MARKET CHART — CROSSHAIR FOLLOWS THE VIEWPORT (TradingView parity), merged to main with the zoom fix.
1. **Root cause:** `project_hover` re-derived the line X from the data-clamped bar index (`x_of(hover.index)`), so past the newest candle the vertical line and the time badge stuck to the last bar.
2. **Fix:** `MarketHover` stores the raw viewport fraction (`x_frac`); the line/badge position comes from the pointer, and bar-facts vs future is decided by the pointer data coordinate. The future area shows only the TIME badge (whole-timeframe stamp shift, no invented OHLC/volume).
3. **Verification:** domain tests `crosshair_follows_the_pointer_into_the_future_area` + `future_area_time_badge_needs_no_invented_facts` + timeframe vocabulary; pixel test `crosshair_keeps_following_the_pointer_into_the_future_area` (proven to fail pre-fix). Also completed the pre-existing `MarketView.bars_stale` projection its Slint binding expects.

**Previous update (371-bug-sweep-COMPLETE, 2026-09-29):** THE ENTIRE 371-BUG HUNT IS FIXED. Six fix waves, all verified green, no commit/push.
1. **Wave 1 (P0, main):** RunProgress wiring + per-leg compare + cancelledΓçÆresults None + protocol guards; Event frozen+slots; full-redact mask/_redact/_mask; READY={LIVE_READY}; side/order strict; OrderFill fill_id/seq; caps legacy; aggregate len/tf/layout-first; bucket_start guards; close_trade ValueError; fetch ProviderError; risk.modelsΓåÆlocal VOs; compiler AST allowlist + guarded import; params finite; lab trade ΓêÆ1ΓåÆNone; spawn fail-closed; event_bus poison-recovery; checked_mul.
2. **Wave 2 (4 parallel teams):** `02_data` 20 (store chmod/traversal/blob-lifetime/rotating loggers/close 15:30/native_download lazy+faults/session s==ok/fatal-fast TOTP/2027 holidays), `05_strategy` 22 (windowed BarView+lookahead ban, registry lock, params proxy, indicators guards, walkforward purge+sort, PBO/Sharpe/DSR fixes, leakage, plot lifecycle, 37 new pins), `06/07/08/09` 23 (every missing argtypes + rc checks, killswitch garbageΓåÆraise, evaluate faultΓåÆdenial, memory atomic+redact, confidence blindΓåÆREDUCED, LRU+locks, faces/caps consistency, funds Optional round-trip, 56ΓåÆ green), `01_core/03_market` 16 (Bar `__post_init__`, loader handshake, **new `core/ai/_vocab.py` un-breaks the whole AI package**, context frozen, locks, 62 new tests, testpaths += 03_market).
3. **Wave 3 (main, `00_app` remainder):** **stdin single-reader redesign** ΓÇö one lifetime reader feeds `_LAB_INBOX`; a run executes in a worker thread while the main loop pumps the same inbox, so cancel lands, deferred commands are RE-QUEUED IN ORDER (`_requeue_deferred`), and idle `cancel_backtest` is handled; idle `cancel_backtest` branch added; `resolve_data_dir` fails closed on a missing EXPLICIT dir (test re-pinned: silently serving another store is the worse bug); broker snapshot no longer writes selection + venue ids come from the registry; broker_manager `_states` under a Lock + `key_field` instead of hardcoded `api_key` + `__error__` no longer overwrites a specific verdict + full `_mask`; `IntervalTimer.stop()` reaps/joins (stopΓåÆstart race) and `WorkerThread` is daemon; `snapshot()` pumps queued ticks so LIVE sessions actually step; live config/quantity/capital strict, corruptΓåÆlog, persist logged not silent, flat-PnL no longer a "win", chart cache key includes last stamp, `market_bars` always a list; market `_row_cache` is mtime-validated, `LIMIT <= 0` returns no rows, parameterized LIMIT, bool prices rejected, `date_range` survives one malformed row (`_scan_bounds`); prefetch polls cancel (serial too) and `shutdown(cancel_futures=True)`; dead `risk_notes` branch removed; research `_number()` coercion so an invalid config yields INVALID instead of crashing.
4. **Wave 4 (`rust/`, team):** chart_math zero-pitch + checked_add + non-finite guards (no `Some(NaN)` into Slint); `bounded_i64` instead of saturating `as i64` (renders `N/A`, never a fake 19-digit number) + `split_rupees` carries the `.100` case; `expect()`s in backtest ΓåÆ Option; scale_frame percent anchor = DATASET first bar (pans no longer re-baseline %); `visible_window` count 0 ΓåÆ `&[]`; set_bars clamps a shrunk dataset; `settings_payload` escapes via serde; bars-stale flag + indicators merge-by-key; `RunProgress::from_json` as_i64 (no NaNΓåÆ0) + `iso_minutes` real calendar validation; download no longer invents 2017-01-01; bridge reads are deadline-bounded with a child-kill watchdog and shutdown can't hang; LabProgress seq-guard; gather capital invalid ΓåÆ None (RUN stays disabled); `--limit` garbage rejected; HOME on non-Windows; paste out-of-range ignored; pending-action drops counted; the 3 dead broker callbacks wired (remove ΓåÆ existing disconnect + honest "credentials still on disk", copy ΓåÆ clipboard crate) with **no invented backend protocol**.
5. **Wave 5 (`scripts/`+config, team):** route symbol regex now sees `pub(crate)/async/trait/mod/type/const/static` + impl methods; ownership `no-loop` catches comprehensions, `no-assign` catches AnnAssign/tuple targets, `importlib`/`__import__` core.native imports caught; authority now also collects UPPER_CASE table authorities, case-insensitive registry maps, bridges detected by CONTENT (16ΓåÆ19 files) with eval/exec/os.system denied; arch gate `~=`-safe, undeclared test-tooling fails, `.sh/.dll/.exe/ΓÇª` denied, **no-git degraded mode instead of exit 2**; scope `--no-cache` actually purges; repo_graph unresolved cap now recorded instead of silently truncating, `pub use` re-exports parsed; context/forensics no-git warn + full-tree scan instead of silent empty scope; Makefile check adds repo-graph, `clean` is cross-platform, `release` guards empty VERSION; CI runs the graph validator; ownership `RUST_PRESENTATION`/`RUST_UI` collapsed into ONE rule (v4); retention `order_state.py` reason corrected to fact + 3 more dead guards found and now a missing guard target is a HARD FAIL; repo_graph regenerated (683 problems ΓåÆ 0, 4297 entities) with a justified cap + 2 new anti-prose guards; benchmark GATE_STEPS now mirrors `make check`.
6. **Wave 6 (reconciliation):** 8 scripts tests pinned a MAGIC line 68 for `class BrokerRegistry` and broke the moment the broker team added a `Lock`. Replaced with derived `_broker_registry_line()` ΓÇö the contract is "the reported line IS the declaration line", which cannot rot.
