**Latest update (lab-config-screenshot-fixes, 2026-09-29):** ┬º02 CONFIG ΓÇö SIX REAL DEFECTS FOUND BY LOOKING AT THE RUNNING APP, NOT BY A TEST (the five ┬º02 render tests were green the whole time).
1. **The tests were green and the screen was still wrong.** A user screenshot exposed what pixel-threshold tests could not: tofu glyphs, elided cell labels, a collapsed progress bar and a nonsense warning. Lesson recorded: a green render test only proves what it asserts ΓÇö it never looked at a glyph, a caption or a text-to-pixel ratio.
2. **GLYPH:** U+2304 `Γîä` is not in the UI font, so every control label rendered `527 STOCKS Γî»`. Replaced with U+25BE `Γû╛` ΓÇö the same block as the `Γû╢` the RUN button already draws successfully.
3. **ELIDED CELLS:** the TIMEFRAME segmented control showed `1... 3... 4... 1h 2h ...` because the column could not fit six cells. `TF_SHORT_CAP` 5 -> 4 and the cell font 10px -> 9.5px, with the column re-weighted 55 -> 78. The brief's rule is the check: a cell wider than its label is a layout bug, and so is a label wider than its cell.
4. **THE COLLAPSED BAR (the real one).** In Slint 1.17 `horizontal-stretch` on a bare `Rectangle` child of a `HorizontalLayout` lays it out at ZERO width, and `width: 100%` inside a stretched layout does the same. A percentage of a width derived from the row that owns the child is a self-reference and resolved to the wrong ancestor (measured 1689px against a 1226px card). The strip is now ABSOLUTE positioned off `cfgcard.width` ΓÇö the ┬º02 width authority that the four columns already use and that is pixel-verified. Three dead ends, all measured, none guessed.
5. **A LIE IN THE CAPTION.** The strip read `22,196 BARS IN 527 STOCKS` ΓÇö that count was the ANCHOR symbol's, never the selection's. Python now sends `anchor` + `symbols_probed`; the note names the symbol (`94,000 BARS IN AAA ┬╖ 24 OF 527 PROBED`) and the caption says `SAMPLED`, not `VERIFIED`, when the probe was bounded.
6. **A NONSENSE WARNING.** `513 symbols < full history` fired because "covering" was tested as `first_bar <= window_start`, and almost no stock has data from 2019. A later listing is not a defect. `covering` now means "has at least one bar inside the window", so the count means what it says: `N symbols have no data in this window`.
7. **A FAILURE WITH NO MESSAGE.** `config_error` was never rendered anywhere in ┬º02 ΓÇö a failed run showed a red border, a red button and `Γ£ò FAILED` and nothing else. The backend's own reason is now the first check row, verbatim. This was the worst of the six: the screen said something was wrong and refused to say what.
8. **Verified:** `cargo test --workspace` all green (397 core, 242 shell, 7 render, every view crate); `cargo fmt --check` clean; 48 Python tests green; `ruff` clean; 5 validators PASS. New test pins the honest caption (`note must name the measured symbol`) and the sampled-vs-verified caption.

**Previous update (lab-run-progress, 2026-09-29):** STRATEGY LAB 527-STOCK RUN - MEASURED BOTTLENECK FIXED (2.3x faster) + REAL LIVE PROGRESS WITH ACCURATE ETA, CURRENT SYMBOL, FAILURE NAMES AND CANCEL (no commit/push).
1. **PROFILED FIRST - the bottleneck was NOT the strategy.** Measured on the real store: `get_bars` = 3419 ms/symbol vs `_run_symbol` = 670 ms/symbol, i.e. **84% of the run was data loading**. `cProfile` then showed **506,562 `datetime.strptime` calls** - 57% of `get_bars`. Root cause: the aggregation loop re-parsed every already-normalised stamp with `strptime` (which re-reads the locale per call), and `_read_rows` fetched the WHOLE table and filtered the date window in Python.
2. **Three exact fixes, zero behaviour change:** (a) `_parse_stamp` slices the fixed ISO shape and only falls back to `strptime` for non-standard rows; (b) the aggregation loop derives the kernel's `days`/`secs` from integer slices instead of building a `datetime` per row; (c) `_read_rows` pushes the window into SQL (`candle_time >= ? AND <= ?`, lexicographic on ISO TEXT, identical to the old Python filter). Plus a `_format_stamp` inverse for the bucket stamps.
3. **MEASURED RESULT (real 527 stocks, OBR, 30m, 2019-09-19 -> 2026-08-18, Rs 10,000 - nothing shortened, skipped or reduced):** **2155s -> 938s (2.3x)**, 527/527 completed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, **55,463 trades and identical metrics before and after**. A 4-worker experiment was measured and REJECTED (item 4).
4. **PARALLELISM: measured, then declined.** Bar loading is a pure read, so it was made parallelisable (`_prefetch_bars`, bounded pool). Benchmarked on this machine (40 symbols, 12 cores): `workers=1 40.7s 1.00x | 2 45.5s 0.89x | 3 50.4s 0.81x | 4 51.9s 0.78x | 6 79.4s 0.51x | 8 73.3s 0.56x`. Loading is CPU-bound Python, so threads only add GIL contention - **the default is 1 worker, and the benchmark is pinned in the docstring AND in a test** so nobody raises it blind. The sequential `_run_symbol` chain is NOT parallelisable by design: each symbol's fill quantity depends on the running equity, so parallelising it would change results.
5. **Real progress, end to end.** New `00_app/app/progress.py` (`RunProgress`) is the only thing that produces the numbers: events are emitted from INSIDE the execution loop after work finished. No ticker, no interpolation - if the backend stops sending, the panel freezes, which is exactly what "stuck" must look like. Transport: the backend streams `{"type":"lab_progress"}` lines on stdout while `run_backtest` blocks; `python_bridge.rs` gained `send_command_streaming` (reads lines, forwards progress, returns the terminal snapshot); `main.rs` applies each event on the UI thread. Every string (elapsed/ETA/speed) is formatted in Rust from the measured payload.
6. **The honesty rules, all tested:** no ETA until `MIN_ETA_SAMPLES` real samples exist (then `-`, never a guess); a slow symbol is flagged `LONG-RUNNING - 00:47` while progress does NOT advance; the LOAD stage has its own real counters so the bar never reads `0 / 527` while a fifth of the read is done; outside a run every duration is `-` (a bare `00:00` would be a fabricated elapsed time). 22 new tests (10 Python progress + 5 Python end-to-end + 7 Rust projection/render).
7. **Fail-fast KEPT, and that is deliberate.** The brief allows stopping on failure only when an existing correctness rule requires it, and forbids returning partial results as complete - this repo does both (`test_insufficient_data_fails_closed`, "insufficient data fails closed"). So a bad symbol still aborts, but the progress event names it, the stage flips to `failed`, and the screen shows how far it got. Weakening that test for per-symbol isolation would have been the dishonest trade.
8. **Cancellation** is cooperative and backend-owned: a `cancel_backtest` command sets a `threading.Event`; a stdin watcher thread services it (and parks any other command so nothing is stolen) while the main loop is blocked. The flag is polled BETWEEN symbols, so a cancel can never leave a half-written result, and a cancelled run is reported `run: "cancelled"` - never as a complete one.
9. **UI:** a 132px live panel above the run bar (RUNNING, `n / N stocks`, %, progress bar, CURRENT symbol, STAGE + its own sub-bar, Completed/Failed/Skipped/Remaining, Elapsed/ETA/throughput, CANCEL RUN, failed-symbol line) in the existing dark style. The RUN button is disabled while a run is in flight (a second RUN would race the equity chain).
10. **Also fixed:** the O(N^2) `sum(t["pnl"] for t in all_trades)` per symbol became a running total with the SAME float accumulation order (identical results, O(1)).
11. **Verified:** `cargo test --workspace` all green (242 shell incl. 7 new progress projection + 2 new render tests, 397 core, all view crates, all render tests); `cargo fmt --check` clean; 47 Python tests green 3x consecutively; `ruff check`/`format` clean; 5 validators PASS; pyright clean on the new files (the remaining `reportMissingImports` are the pre-existing stale-editable-install break recorded below).
12. **NOT DONE / honest gaps:** the brief's `bars-processed / CPU / RAM` metrics are omitted - the run is a separate process and nothing measures them safely; **no checkpointing**, because the equity chain makes symbols sequentially dependent, so a resume must replay the same prefix and would save no correct work; no automatic retry (a failed symbol is a correctness stop, not a transient blip, and retrying it risks a silent retry loop).

**Previous update (dead-code-sweep, 2026-09-29):** DEAD CODE REMOVED ΓÇö repo-wide sweep, 13 definitions + 5 Rust warnings (no commit/push; this checkout has NO `.git`, so the "live-tree scope" tooling below is dead here). Net effect: `cargo check --workspace --all-targets` went from **5 warnings to ZERO**.
1. **What was actually dead (proved, not guessed).** I built a token-count scan over the whole repo (`.py/.rs/.slint/.toml/.md/.json`) ΓÇö a name is dead only if it occurs EXACTLY ONCE corpus-wide (its own definition). That killed 13: `ERR_RATE_LIMITED` (`02_data/data/provider/contract.py` ΓÇö the only one of 7 `ERR_*` codes no adapter or test ever raises), `_POINT_TYPES` + `_SPAN_TYPES` (`strategy/models/plot_event.py` ΓÇö orphaned halves of the point/span plot split), `_CLOSE_RECORDED` (`backtest/native_positions.py`), `_state_by_code` (`execution/native_order_state.py`), `_CHAR_OUT` (`execution/market_data/native_normalizer.py`), `FINAL_STATES` (`scripts/failure_intel.py`), `SAFETY_INVARIANTS` (`scripts/self_opt.py`), `SCOPE_SCHEMA` (`scripts/validate_scope.py`), `TREND_LABELS` (`scripts/vayren_intel.py`), `SIZE` (`scripts/assets/make_icon.py` ΓÇö `main()` hardcodes `[16ΓÇª256]`), `ACTION_WRITE` (`scripts/forensics/analysis.py`), `_ANCHOR_ACTIONS` (`scripts/forensics/intervals.py`).
2. **Rust: 1 dead fn + 4 warnings, all removed.** `research_state.rs::st_symbols` (compiler-proven `never used` ΓÇö an orphaned test helper left by the `field("symbols")` rename); `lab.slint` `padding: 16px` sitting on a bare `Rectangle` in the trade-detail panel (Slint: *"padding only has effect on layout elements"* ΓÇö a silently-ignored property); `system-view/tests/shot.rs` an `unsafe` block the compiler proved unnecessary; `lab_scroll.rs` parens around an index expr; `strategy-lab-view/src/lib.rs:920` a `let mut state` that is only read.
3. **WHAT I DELIBERATELY DID NOT DELETE ΓÇö and why this matters.** Vulture + a naive scan flag ~200 more "unused" names. Almost all are **public API surface of a deliberately unwired module**, not garbage: `AI_ENTRY.md` ┬º2/┬º4 and `90_brain/architecture.md` stage these as contract-first (e.g. the plot DSL in `strategies/base.py` ΓÇö `plot_line`/`plot_ray`/ΓÇª are unreferenced in-repo but are what the user's real `D:\VAYREN_STRATEGIES` `.py` files call; the 4 sibling `surface_*` queries on the LIVE `broker_selection_service`; the `native_*` bridges). Deleting those is an architecture decision, not a cleanup. `ruff`'s configured rule set is ALREADY dead-code-clean: `F401/F811/F841/ARG` all report nothing, so there were no unused imports, unused locals, or unused parameters anywhere. Zero dead Slint callbacks (all ~230 are invoked from Rust).
4. **One real latent bug found, NOT fixed (needs a pixel decision, not a cleanup):** that ignored `padding: 16px` was almost certainly *meant* to inset the trade-detail panel ΓÇö its inner `VerticalLayout` has `spacing: 0` and no padding, so the panel renders flush. I removed the dead property (rendering unchanged, since Slint ignored it); moving it onto the `VerticalLayout` would be a real layout change and belongs to whoever owns that screen.
5. **Verified:** `pytest` (chapter `PYTHONPATH`, excluding the 2 known-stale files) **638 passed / 2 failed**; `cargo test -p vayren-shell -p vayren-system-view -p vayren-strategy-lab-view` **all green** (235 shell lib + 5 `lab_scroll` + 1 `lab_select` + 1 `live_render_snapshot` + 1 `render_app` + 1 `render_matrix` + 1 `lab_config_render` + system shot + strategy-lab render 7); `cargo check --workspace --all-targets` **0 warnings**; `rustfmt` clean on all 4 touched files; `ruff check`/`format` clean (247 files); validators **structure / imports / language-ownership (247 files) / authority / routes / context-check** all PASS.
6. **The 6 remaining failures in this tree are ALL environmental, none from this change ΓÇö PROVEN, not assumed.** (a) `validate_architecture_gate.py` and the 4 `test_validate_scope::test_bench_*` cases need `git diff HEAD`, and **there is no `.git` here** ΓåÆ scope `''`/UNKNOWN (same class as the freshness failure recorded below). (b) `test_vayren_intel::test_scan_never_fabricates` asserts a real git revision ΓåÆ `None`. (c) `test_repo_graph` ├ù2: the committed `90_brain/repo_graph.json` is dated **Sept 26** while the tree was edited Sept 29 ΓÇö it does not even contain the `data_bounds` key, so the artifact was already stale; the second failure is a hardcoded 2.15 MB prose cap. (d) `test_fyers_provider::test_reload_credentials_falls_back_to_layered_loader` ΓÇö the ambient Fyers-credential failure already recorded. (e) `pyright` 135├ù`reportMissingImports`: `vayren-core` is **not pip-installed from this root** (`import core` ΓåÆ `None`); `pip install -e . --no-deps` here fixes it. Its 4 real type errors (`headless.py:844` `progress.completed` on `None`, `backtest_service.py:295/307`) are pre-existing in files this task never touched.

**Previous update (lab-config-02-redesign, 2026-09-29):** LAB ┬º02 CONFIGURE ΓÇö REBUILT TO THE REFERENCE MOCK'S STRUCTURE, WITH EVERY INVENTED NUMBER REMOVED (no commit/push). Note: this ran in the `vayren-core-main` checkout (the prompt's `strategy-layer-final` path does not exist on this machine).
1. **Structure (┬º1162-1366 of `ui/lab.slint` replaced):** the bare `LabEyebrow` is now a `LabStepRail` (`02` badge + title + hairline + the neighbouring steps), and the card is ONE three-row rhythm across four columns ΓÇö label 19 / control 36 / sub 30, gaps 12 and 15, body height `LabPalette.cfg-col-h` (112px). The baseline is structural, not four hand-tuned paddings, and `tests/lab_config_render.rs` proves it by scanning four rendered columns for a shared 36px control band.
2. **A layout trap worth remembering:** `horizontal-stretch` on a bare `Rectangle` inside a `HorizontalLayout` was NOT distributed as specified ΓÇö the columns came out 115/232/619/260 instead of 105:55:138:62, because a weight is only spread over the space the controls do not already demand, and the DATE RANGE control's minimum width silently stole a column's share. Fix: explicit fractions (`width: cfgrow.width * 105 / 360`). Same trap killed the completeness track: a stretched `Rectangle` with no width collapsed to 0px, so the bar vanished; it now lives inside its own stretched `HorizontalLayout`. A `Text` with `horizontal-alignment: right` and no `width` renders at x=0 ΓÇö it must be given `x` + `width` or it lands on top of its own label.
3. **Honest substitutions (mock ΓåÆ real):** `MIN Γé╣5,000` + `NO LEVERAGE` ΓåÆ the one rule the kernel enforces, `CAPITAL > 0` (from `backtest_validation.rs` bit 4, shown with its real Γ£ô/Γ£ò); `Est. runtime ~1.4 s` ΓåÆ DROPPED (nothing in lab state ever measured a run duration); `Bars to scan` ΓåÆ real `UNIVERSE` / `CAPITAL` facts derived from the committed input, never the stale `config.capital` echo; `94.2%` ΓåÆ an integer percent (Slint 1.17 cannot format a float); blue `#4c8dff` ΓåÆ `LabPalette.lab-teal`.
4. **Date-range presets are driven by REAL store bounds, not the selection.** New snapshot key `data_bounds` (`_lab_workspace` sends the anchor symbol's real `first_date`/`last_date`). Deriving `MAX` from `cfg_dates_start/end` as the brief suggested would have collapsed `MAX` onto whatever the user last picked; the bounds are captured separately for that reason. `range_presets_for(first, last)` returns exactly 5 cells, all ending at the last available bar, and a cell whose nominal span reaches before the first available bar is CLAMPED and relabelled with the span the data really has (`10Y` ΓåÆ `"6.9 YRS"` on a 6.9-year store). A click reports an INDEX and `interaction_range_preset` rewrites the committed ISO bounds through the EXISTING `interaction_dates` ΓÇö no second date pipeline; Slint still never parses a date.
5. **Coverage is now a real, measured strip.** New kernel `rust/vayren-core/src/lab_coverage.rs` (`coverage_facts`, `expected_bars`, reusing `download.rs`'s weekday rule, 9 tests). New `lab_coverage` backend command: Python COUNTS (`date_range()` per symbol + `get_bars()` for the anchor) and Rust derives the expectation and the percentage from the same window ΓÇö so the number has exactly one owner. Default probe is the first 24 selected symbols and the screen SAYS `┬╖ SAMPLE OF 24`; a full scan needs the explicit `full` flag. `expected == 0` ΓåÆ `measured == false` ΓåÆ the strip is hidden, never a fake `0%`.
6. **ANIMATION PATH USED: integer count-up WORKS in Slint 1.17.** `animate cov-shown { duration: 900ms; easing: ease-out; }` on an `<int>` property compiles and the number counts up. The bar fill animates its `width` (a length). Both were verified by building, not assumed. The software-renderer tests assert the FINAL percent from the model ΓÇö the harness has no vsync, so no test may look at a mid-animation frame.
7. **Two real consistency bugs the render caught (not the tests):** the TIMEFRAME control showed `config.timeframe` while the segmented row highlighted `timeframes[index]`, so they could disagree ΓÇö both now read a new `timeframe_label` resolved from the ladder; and the run bar's `CAPITAL` fact showed the stale `config.capital` (`Γé╣10,00,000`) while the field showed the committed `10000` ΓÇö it now shows a new `capital_label` built from the same input.
8. **Verified:** `cargo test --workspace` all targets green (397 core incl. 9 new coverage, 235 shell incl. 14 new ┬º02, new `lab_config_render` 5/5, all 6 view crates, all 5 render tests); `cargo fmt --all --check` clean; 8 new `00_app` coverage-probe tests pass; `ruff check`/`format` clean; 6 validators PASS; `pyright` clean for the new code (the 4 remaining `reportMissingImports` are the pre-existing stale-editable-install break recorded below). Palette: only 2 genuinely new tokens (`cfg-col-h`, `cfg-sub-h`); the brief's other three were value-duplicates of `soft2`/`field-bg`/`teal-dim` and were deliberately NOT added.
9. **NOT DONE / honest gaps:** the "gaps filled" counter is OMITTED (nothing counts interior holes cheaply and honestly); `expected_bars` models MonΓÇôFri sessions with no holiday table, so the percent is a documented upper-bound denominator, not an audited figure.

**Previous update (lab-trade-blotter-virtual, 2026-09-28):** LAB TRADES TAB ΓÇö BUG FIXED (it never rendered trades at all) + THE BLOTTER IS NOW A VIRTUALIZED INDIVIDUAL-TRADE TABLE (no commit/push).
1. **ROOT CAUSE of "Trades shows stock summary rows" (traced, not guessed):** `root.trades` was declared in `lab.slint` and Rust pushed real rows into it (`set_lab_trades`), but **zero slint elements ever read it** ΓÇö the trades table was never built. The lens tab bar only recolors itself (`root.lab.lens == idx` on the tab), it never gated content, so clicking Trades left the page on section 04 INVESTIGATE, which IS the per-stock ranking grid. The data path itself was fine: `backtest_service.py:364-374` emits the real records (`symbol, side, entry_time, exit_time, entry_px, exit_px, pnl, r_multiple, bars, reason, winning`) and `parse_trade_row` mapped them. No quantity, no per-trade return % and no per-trade strategy exist in the schema, so the blotter shows NONE of them (only real fields, `spec ┬º2`).
2. Two real defects fixed on the way: (a) `shown_trades` CLONED the whole trade list on every single projection (O(trades) per state change) ΓÇö now the UI receives only the window; (b) clicking a row queued `tradesel:`/`tradefocus:` for a backend that implements neither, so a click had no visible effect and the detail could never open ΓÇö selection is now an optimistic local echo, and the detail is built from the one clicked record (`interaction_trade_pick` + `trade_detail_for`).
3. **One window engine, two tables** (`spec ┬º36/┬º40`): `RankWindow` became `VirtualWindow` (row height, overscan, scroll, thumb, index, invalidation key are all fields now) and both the ranking grid and the trade blotter use it. `VirtualWindowView` replaced `RankWindowView`; `RankWindow`/`RankWindowView` remain as aliases so existing code and tests are untouched. Trade rows cache `sort: [f64; 4]` (entry minutes, P&L, R, bars) parsed once, so filtering and sorting are numeric and never touch display strings.
4. Blotter behavior: search (stock OR exit reason, allocation-free ASCII match), side filter (all/long/short), result filter (all/winners/losers), sort by entry time / P&L / R / bars / stock ΓÇö all over the COMPLETE dataset, re-sorting only when the sort changes and re-filtering (never re-sorting) on a keystroke. Found and fixed a latent bug in the shared engine while doing it: a RELAXED filter (cleared search, "All sides") could only narrow the already-filtered view, so clearing a search did not bring the rows back ΓÇö now scored by restrictiveness and rebuilt from identity when it relaxes.
5. Slint work: a real trade page (`04 ┬╖ TRADE BLOTTER ΓÇö EVERY INDIVIDUAL TRADE`) with 11 columns on a bounded viewport, sticky two-row header, windowed rows, real scrollbar with a draggable thumb, wheel + drag scroll, a tools row (honest count "412 trades ┬╖ showing 401-427", search, side, result, sort) and a selected-trade detail panel. The lens now routes: `lens == 1` shows the blotter and hides the stock summary (they are two different questions, not a filter of one table); every other lens is unchanged, so no view was redesigned.
6. **MEASURED (`perf_trades.rs`, debug build, this machine):** `project()` with a trade blotter 1k=0.088ms / 10k=0.088ms / 100k=0.092ms ΓÇö FLAT; scroll 600 notches over 100k = 0.115ms mean (max 1.9ms); keystroke filter at 100k = 22ms mean (O(N), no re-sort); criterion change at 100k = 153ms (user-initiated, debug). **1,000,000 synthetic trades through the real parse path**: ingest 21.1s (once per snapshot, debug, 7 `format!` per trade), `project()` **0.65ms**, Γëñ42 rows rendered, mid-scroll works. HONEST LIMITATION (spec ┬º35): the production trade count is whatever one universe run produces (hundreds to low thousands) ΓÇö the 1M/3M numbers are a STRESS construction, not a claim about the real dataset, and ingest is the cost that would dominate at that scale.
7. **Previous update (lab-ranking-virtual-table, 2026-09-28):** LAB RANKING TABLE ΓÇö PRODUCTION-GRADE VIRTUALIZED DATA GRID (no commit/push). Spec was DOM/React-flavoured; this is its native Slint/Rust equivalent, architecture unchanged (`FULL DATA -> index layer (filter+sort on u32 indices) -> visible range -> adaptive overscan -> rendered rows`).
1. **PROFILED FIRST** (new `rust/vayren-shell/src/perf_table.rs`, `#![cfg(test)]`, mirrors the chart's `perf.rs`): BEFORE ΓÇö `project()` 1k=1.67ms / 10k=14.9ms / ~75ms extrapolated, filter+sort at 50k=**71ms** (4.4├ù the 16ms frame budget). The cause was NOT rendering: `project()` cloned the WHOLE `LabResults` per frame (`results_or_default`) and re-split a 50k-symbol universe CSV twice per frame (`universe_count` + `universe_chips`).
2. Fixes (all measured, no visual change): `results_or_default` now returns `&LabResults` (static `OnceLock` empty), the universe COUNT is derived where the CSV is adopted (`cfg_universe_count`), and CHIPS became a presentation cap (`UNIVERSE_CHIP_CAP = 6`, fed from `universe_selected`) ΓÇö the count label still states the true total. `RankRow` gained `sort: [f64; 7]` cached keys.
3. Virtual window (`lab::RankWindow`): `view: Vec<u32>` (order), `first`/`count` (window), `scroll_px`, `viewport_h`, `velocity`, and a `RankViewKey` (rows + search + criterion + desc) for invalidation. The index is rebuilt ONLY on data arrival (`apply_parity_keys`), engine results (`apply_result`) or a filter/sort edit ΓÇö never per frame, never per scroll notch. A keystroke filters the existing order instead of re-sorting it, so typing never reorders what the user reads. Sort is a `Vec<(f64, u32)>` key array with an index tiebreaker (deterministic, no row clones, no per-comparison indirection); "ΓÇö" rows get ┬▒Γê₧ so they sink without a branch. **`RANKING_VIEW_CAP = 50` is GONE ΓÇö nothing is truncated**; `spec ┬º22` (no pagination/hiding) is now structurally impossible to reintroduce accidentally.
4. `spec ┬º2/┬º9` adaptive quality: overscan = 2 idle ΓÇª 8 on a fast flick, from the measured velocity (`bench_fast_flick_ΓÇª` proves idle=12 / flick=26 / settled=14 rows). `┬º3` zero full-table re-render: the band is signed (FNV over the window, split hi/lo because Slint's `int` is i32) and `apply_lab` skips the whole `set_lab_ranking` push when the signature is unchanged. `┬º12`: fixed 40px rows, sticky header outside the scrolled band, viewport height + scrollbar thumb derived in Rust. `┬º11`: clamped scroll, thumb inside the track, no overshoot.
5. Slint work: the panel became a bounded viewport (`height: lab-rank-window.viewport-h`) with the row band positioned at `50px + i*row-h - scroll-px`; wheel (`scroll-event`, 120px notches) + drag scroll (`pointer-event` down/move/up, same idiom as `market.slint`) + a draggable scrollbar thumb; new `RankScrollState` gesture component; new `RankWindow` struct exported through `app.slint`. Row click still opens the inspector.
6. AFTER (same benches, same machine, debug build): `project()` 1k=**0.026ms** 10k=**0.031ms** 50k=**0.043ms** (FLAT ΓÇö the point of the whole exercise); scroll+project 600 notches = **0.048ms** mean / 0.28ms max; keystroke filter at 50k = **3.96ms** mean (no re-sort); sort change at 50k = **26.8ms** mean (the one deliberate O(N log N), user-initiated, debug build ΓÇö release is several times faster). Ingest unchanged by design (`apply_snapshot` 10k = ~42ms: every cell is formatted once, on arrival).
7. Tests: 7 perf benches + 2 new correctness tests ΓÇö `virtual_window_shows_every_row_without_rendering_them_all` (walks a 500-row dataset viewport-by-viewport; union == dataset, order 0499ΓåÆ0000, Γëñ40 rows rendered) and `virtual_window_geometry_is_exact_and_never_negative`. `cargo test -p vayren-shell --lib` **203 passed**; all 5 integration targets green EXCEPT the pre-existing `lab_select::research_object_rows_are_fully_visible_and_unobstructed` (the concurrent session's 58px top-bar removal shifts that test's fixed pixel window ΓÇö see the entry below). `cargo fmt` clean; ownership/structure/imports validators PASS; exe rebuilt and relaunched through `VAYREN.lnk`.
8. NOT DONE / honest gaps: no off-main-thread split (the sort is 26ms worst case in a DEBUG build and happens on an explicit user action ΓÇö the backend is already a separate process, and moving a 26ms sort off-thread would cost more than it saves); no prefetching (the dataset is local ΓÇö the spec forbids pointless network work); no debounce on the search box (a keystroke is 4ms, under budget). No new dependency was added (`spec ┬º23`): std + existing Slint.

**Previous update (lab-ranking-reference-table-inspector, 2026-09-28):** LAB SECTION 04 ΓÇö RANKING TABLE REBUILT TO THE HTML REFERENCE + WORKING PER-STOCK INSPECTOR (no commit/push). Reference: `C:\Users\visha\Downloads\000(1) (7) (2).html` (the legacy preview the Lab ports 1:1).
1. Root cause of the squeezed table: `LabTh`/`LabTd` bound `width: root.tw`, and a bound `width` wins over the layout ΓÇö so every `stretch: 1` metric column (Net P&L, Return, Trades, Win %, PF, Max DD, Sharpe) was pinned at `tw = 0px` and collapsed; only `#` (34px) + `STOCK` (110px) survived, which is exactly the "ranking not showing" screenshot. Fix: `preferred-width: root.tw` + `horizontal-stretch: root.stretch`, so fixed columns reserve space and the metrics share the rest. These two components are used ONLY by the ranking table.
2. The inspector was unreachable DEAD UI: `on_lab_rank_picked` only queued `ranksel:<symbol>`, which NOTHING consumes (no backend action queue, no `detail` key anywhere in `headless.py`) ΓÇö so `state.detail` was always None and `insp-open` (`lab.detail-symbol != ""`) never true. Fix: `LabState::interaction_rank_picked` derives the whole drawer from the clicked `RankRow` (symbol, P&L, caption `MODE ┬╖ timeframe ┬╖ return`, and the 6 reference metrics Return/Trades/Win rate/PF/Max DD/Sharpe) ΓÇö no round-trip, no invented contract, no empty drawer for an unknown symbol. Drawer P&L bumped to 26px to match the reference's `.big`.
3. Table behaviour the reference had and the native screen did not: the search box now FILTERS rows (it only echoed before) and the sort control REORDERS them (only a header arrow before), renumbering `#` after the reorder; "ΓÇö" (unranked) rows always sink; the cap applies after filtering so a search can never be starved. Criterion ΓåÆ column mapping is derived from the existing `RANKBY_HEADER` (no duplicated table) and an out-of-range box is an honest no-sort.
4. `RankRow` gained `sort: [f64; 7]` raw keys (display strings like "ΓÇö" vs "+4.2%" cannot be compared honestly). Footer line follows the reference: "6 stocks analyzed ┬╖ inspecting MESOLAR" while the drawer is open.
5. Tests: 2 new (`ranking_search_filters_and_criterion_sorts_then_renumbers`, `rank_pick_opens_the_inspector_from_the_row_itself`). `cargo test -p vayren-shell --lib` 194 passed; all integration targets green EXCEPT `lab_select::research_object_rows_are_fully_visible_and_unobstructed` ΓÇö that pixel test scans a FIXED y-window (150..320) in the 01 DEFINE section and the CONCURRENT session's 58px top-bar removal shifts content out of it (found a 15px sliver instead of two full rows). It is not caused by this change: `LabTh`/`LabTd` appear only in the ranking table, and no section-01 element was touched here. That session owns the top-bar edit and must re-pin/re-run that test. `cargo fmt` clean; exe rebuilt (1m51s) and relaunched through `VAYREN.lnk` (window up, one backend).

Transaction cost removed end-to-end (user asked "pura hata do"): `ui/lab.slint` field block + `cfg-cost`/`cfg-cost-warn` props + `cost-committed` callback; `ui/app.slint` `lab-cost-committed`; `lab.rs` `cfg_cost`/`cfg_cost_warn` state + view props, `interaction_cost`, the 0%-warn derivation, the receipt's "cost X%/side" clause, `run_key()`'s cost segment, 2 tests; `shell.rs` `LabRunRequest.cost`, `LabSelectRequest.cost`, `parse_cost_pct` + its test; `python_bridge.rs` `cost` on both Lab commands (+ JSON pinned in 2 tests); `main.rs` call sites; `headless.py` stops parsing/passing `cost`. **Previous update (dependency-tree-optimization, 2026-09-28, from main via d9a76fe / PR #15):** DEPENDENCY TREE OPTIMIZATION & WORKSPACE UNIFICATION (merged to `main`; brought into `strategy-layer-final` by the sync merge ΓÇö record kept verbatim).

   - Evaluated all direct & transitive dependencies across Python (`pyproject.toml`) and Rust (`rust/Cargo.toml` and 8 member crates).

All retained with 0 functionality loss.
2. Workspace Dependency Consolidation:

   - Pinned `slint` to `~1.17.0` (with `default-features = false` centrally, `vayren-shell` enabling `default-features = true`), safely guarding against unconstrained `cargo update` pulling in Slint 1.18's heavy `wgpu` / Vulkan / DirectX stack.

**Previous update (market-chart-1600-viewport, 2026-09-27):** MARKET CHART ΓÇö 1600-CANDLE HARD VIEWPORT CAP + CURSOR-ANCHORED ZOOM + CONTINUOUS (f64) PAN. Branch `antigravity-branch` ΓåÆ merged to `main` via squash PR #13 (governance; explicit user command "github per main me merge karo"). SUPERSEDES main's concurrent `market-chart-zoom-anchor-fix` (72b6bdd, PlotX architecture ΓÇö kept below as record): same root causes, this implementation is the superset (adds the 1600 cap + f64 `first` as the ONLY origin state + grid/red-line removal + 7 tests Γëê their 5); rebase resolution kept their `rust/Cargo.toml` `[profile.dev]/[profile.test] debug = 1` (MSVC linker OOM guard) + analysis.py tolerance, reverted their shell.rs act_vp2 `origin` hunk (no separate `origin` field here; f64 `first` compare is exact). All changes Rust-only (`rust/vayren-shell/src/market.rs`); shell.rs/market.slint/app.slint changes are the grid-removal entry below (act_vp2 f64-compatible; slint already sends unrounded fractions; plot `clip:true` handles partial edge candles).
1. Root causes (3): (a) EXCESSIVE VISIBILITY ΓÇö `MAX_VISIBLE_BARS` was 10000, so zoom-out rendered up to 10k candles/frame; (b) ZOOM YANK/JUMP ΓÇö `WheelZoom` clamped `new_first_raw.min(anchor_first(...))` re-anchoring a view parked in empty right space every step (THE "chart slides during zoom" bug) + integer `round_py(first)`; (c) PAN SNAP/STUTTER ΓÇö integer `first: usize` rounded per gesture, swallowing sub-bar deltas ΓåÆ candle-to-candle jumping.
2. Fix: `MAX_VISIBLE_BARS = 1600` (VIEWPORT cap only ΓÇö doc explicitly states full history stays in memory, reachable by pan, nothing deleted/truncated/downsampled/aggregated). `pub first: f64` = single floating-point source of truth. Canonical transform pair `x_of(idx)=((idx-first)+0.5)/slots` (dataΓåÆscreen) + `index_at(x)=floor(first+x*slots)` clamped (screenΓåÆdata) used by candles, time ticks, series points, trade markers, strategy markers, rays, crosshair ΓÇö one coordinate authority. `clamp_first(0..=max_pan_first)`, `first_floor()`, `at_live_edge()` (┬╝-bar sticky). WheelZoom: exact fractional anchor `first+fraction*count`, clamp unified with pan (`clamp_first`, never re-yanks right-space-parked view), `follow_latest=at_live_edge()`. **Previous update (market-chart-zoom-anchor-fix, 2026-09-27):** (SUPERSEDED by market-chart-1600-viewport above for market.rs/shell.rs architecture ΓÇö kept as the record of what landed on main as 72b6bdd; its Cargo.toml debug profiles + analysis.py tolerance REMAIN in effect.) MARKET CANDLESTICK CHART ZOOM SMOOTHNESS / ANCHOR FIX.
1. Root causes resolved:
   - Integer discretization in `MarketState::first: usize` caused $\pm 0.5$ slot rounding jumps on every wheel notch and accumulated drift.
   - Fatal `.min(self.anchor_first(total, new_count))` clamp in `WheelZoom` forcibly snapped viewports back to the live edge during historical zooms.
   - Disjoint coordinate calculations between candles, time ticks, crosshair, and overlays caused edge jitter.
2. Architecture & Mathematical Solution (`rust/vayren-shell/src/market.rs`, `rust/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs`):
   - Introduced `PlotX` struct as the single source of truth for horizontal data $\leftrightarrow$ screen coordinates ($f_x = (\text{bar} - \text{origin} + 0.5)/\text{slots}$; inverse $\text{bar} = \text{origin} + f_x \cdot \text{slots} - 0.5$).
   - Exact zoom transform preserving cursor anchor at $f_x$: $\text{new\_origin} = \text{origin} + f_x \cdot (\text{count} - \text{new\_count})$, clamped to `0.0..=self.max_origin()`. Reversibility proven: 10 in + 10 out yields zero drift ($< 10^{-6}$).
   - `MarketState` holds continuous `origin: f64` while keeping `first: usize` in sync (`round_py(origin)`) for full backward compatibility with tests.
   - Unified rendering: candles, time ticks, crosshair hover snap, plot series, ray levels, trade markers, and strategy markers all project through `PlotX`.
   - `act_vp2!` in `rust/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs` detects continuous sub-bar changes `(st.origin - prev_origin).abs() > 1e-6` to trigger viewport redraws.
   - `rust/Cargo.toml` configured with `[profile.dev]` and `[profile.test]` `debug = 1` to prevent MSVC linker OOM on large Slint ASTs.
3. Verification:
   - All 62 unit tests in `rust/vayren-shell` passed (5 new dedicated zoom anchor tests: `zoom_anchor_preserves_coordinate_at_left_center_right`, `zoom_reversibility_zero_cumulative_drift`, `historical_zoom_does_not_snap_to_live_edge`, `live_edge_zoom_preserves_anchor_under_cursor`, `continuous_sub_bar_panning_and_hover_alignment`).
   - `python scripts/validate_language_ownership.py` PASSED (244 files). Local only, no git commit/push.

**Previous update (tradingview-crosshair-and-performance-master-fix, 2026-09-25):** TRADINGVIEW-STYLE CROSSHAIR & SMOOTHNESS / PERFORMANCE MASTER FIX.
1. Architecture & Core Root Cause Fixes:
   - Unified 2D Coordinate System ($X = \text{time}$, $Y = \text{price}$): Crosshair tracks continuous normalized coordinates `x_frac` and `y_frac`. Vertical and horizontal hairlines render continuously across candle space without discrete candle-center jumping or artificial snapping.
   - Synchronized OHLCV Header: `header_ohlc` displays the exact OHLC values corresponding to the hovered candle. When pointer moves into empty right margin or exits plot (`HoverLeft`), values cleanly fall back to latest bar without freezing stale historical figures.
   - Realtime In-Place Updates: `refresh_crosshair(&mut self)` re-evaluates hover from stored `(x_frac, y_frac)` on tick arrival (`update_or_append_bar`), zooming (`WheelZoom`), and panning (`DragMove`, `WheelPanX`), preventing cursor-freeze and keeping stationary hover values refreshed in-place.
   - Performance Tiering (60+ FPS): Separated pointer tracking (`on_hover_moved` -> `apply_hover`) into an ultra-fast zero-allocation path updating only 8 scalar properties (< 0.05ms) without reallocating vector models, candles, segments, or watchlist rows. Wheel zoom, pan, and price drag route through `apply_viewport` (plot geometry only), reserving full state reapplication for dataset changes.
   - Scale Mode Parity: Transformed price hover and tick generation accurately project across Regular, Percent, and Logarithmic scale modes.
2. Full 22-Point Regression Test Suite (`rust/vayren-shell/src/market.rs` Section 30):
   - All 22 regression points implemented and PASSING:
     1. Pointer resolves correct timestamp across intraday/daily/weekly.

     7. Realtime tick on same forming bar updates OHLC readout in-place.
     8. Realtime tick creating next bar transitions cleanly.

     10. Horizontal panning with stationary mouse preserves crosshair coordinates.
     11. Intraday crosshair time format ("2 Jan 2024 09:15").
     12. Daily crosshair time format ("2 Jan 2024").

**Previous update (tradingview-chart-parity-and-time-scale, 2026-09-25):** TRADINGVIEW-STYLE FINANCIAL CHART & CANONICAL TIME-SERIES PIPELINE OVERHAUL.
1. Root Cause Diagnosed & Proven:
   - Time Axis Discontinuity: `rust/vayren-shell/src/market.rs` previously sampled ticks by uniform index math `idx = ((k + 0.5) * visible / max_ticks).floor()`, causing sampled bars to land on arbitrary fractions of candle arrays rather than canonical time boundaries.
   - Intraday Label Truncation Bug: `format_axis_time` unconditionally stripped the date for intraday bars and output only `time_part[..5]` (`HH:MM`). When a visible window spanned multiple days, this produced the reported backward jumps: e.g., 14:45 on Day 1, 14:30 on Day 2, 09:15 on Day 3.
   - Missing Sorting & Deduplication: `set_bars` did not parse timestamps or deduplicate timestamps deterministically, leaving raw SQLite input susceptible to duplicate visual candles.
   - Crosshair Snapping: Crosshair forced snapping `hover.index` onto candle centers for the vertical line, preventing continuous cursor tracking.
   - Same-series Price Manual Reset Bug: In `set_bars`, `price_manual` was previously reset to `None` on every snapshot even when `same_series` was true, causing any manual price pan/drag or zoomed scaling to snap back to default auto-fit when the same symbol and timeframe received an update.
2. Architectural Fixes & Implementations:
   - Canonical Time Architecture (`rust/vayren-core/src/market.rs`): Added zero-dependency `CanonicalDateTime` struct (`epoch_seconds`, `year`, `month`, `day`, `hour`, `minute`, `second`) utilizing Howard Hinnant's civil calendar algorithm; added `parse_canonical_timestamp(raw: &str) -> Option<CanonicalDateTime>` supporting ISO 8601 strings; added `BarValidationError` and `validate_bars(bars: &[Bar], timeframe: &str) -> Result<(), BarValidationError>`.
   - Python Market Validation & Deduplication (`03_market/market/models/bar.py`, `00_app/app/services/market_data_service.py`): Exported Python `validate_bars(bars, timeframe) -> list[str]`. Updated `_read_rows` and `get_bars` in `MarketDataService` to deterministically merge duplicate timestamps (`max(high)`, `min(low)`, latest `close`, max `volume`).
   - Shell View-Model & Adaptive Time Scale (`rust/vayren-shell/src/market.rs`):
     - Updated `MarketState::set_bars` to sort strictly ascending by parsed epoch seconds and merge consecutive identical timestamps deterministically. Retained `price_manual` across snapshots when `same_series` is true (resetting only on `!same_series` when switching symbol/timeframe), preventing viewport price snap-backs.
     - Added `MarketState::update_or_append_bar(&mut self, bar: MarketBar) -> bool` handling same-bar realtime modifications, new-bar transitions, and chronological out-of-order packets.
     - Implemented `generate_time_ticks`: adaptive TradingView-style time scale with day-boundary priority (`Jun 10`), hourly marks within day (`10:00`, `11:00`), multi-day scales, and macro year/month scales, strictly enforcing non-collision and strictly monotonic positions.
     - Refined crosshair: `MarketHover` and `MarketAction::HoverMoved` now record `x_frac: f32`, letting the vertical crosshair line smoothly follow pointer position while resolving the underlying candle OHLC and timestamp.
3. Regression Test Suite:
   - A. Chronological 15m bars (`test_scenario_a_chronological_bars_sorting`): scrambled multi-day input sorted ascending.
   - B. Duplicate reconciliation (`test_scenario_b_duplicate_timestamp_reconciliation`): duplicate timestamps merge without duplicate visual candles.
   - C. Crosshair resolution (`test_scenario_c_crosshair_resolution_and_ohlc`): hover X resolves exact candle, timestamp, volume, and OHLC.
   - D. Timeframe aggregation bucket ordering (`test_scenario_d_timeframe_aggregation_bucket_ordering`): validates 15m, 1h, 1D monotonic bucket ordering.
   - E. Realtime same-bar update (`test_scenario_e_realtime_same_bar_update`): updates open bar in place without shifting chart.
   - F. Realtime next-bar transition (`test_scenario_f_realtime_next_bar_transition`): appends exactly 1 new bar at next canonical slot.
   - G. Out-of-order realtime packet (`test_scenario_g_out_of_order_realtime_packet`): late packets update or insert in chronological order.
   - H. Zoom/pan stability (`test_scenario_h_zoom_pan_preserves_scale_and_candle_identity`): preserves scale, minimum spacing, and candle mapping.
   - I. Session boundary handling (`test_scenario_i_session_boundary_handling`): overnight/weekend gaps do not generate fake bars.
   - J. Date boundary transitions (`test_scenario_j_date_boundary_transitions`): multi-day transitions smoothly display date markers without backward time jumps.
   - Full 9/9 C ABI and render matrix tests in `rust/vayren-market-view/tests/render_market.rs` (including free one-sided pan without snap-back, parity markers, and responsive tiers).

**Previous update (fyers-auth-fix-and-broker-icons, 2026-09-23):** FYERS API V3 AUTHENTICATION FIX & BROKER UI POLISH.
1. Root cause diagnosed & fixed: FYERS `POST /api/v3/token` endpoint returns HTTP 308 (Permanent Redirect) with the authorization code inside the response body (`{"Url":"http://.../vayren/fyers-callback?s=ok&code=200&auth_code=..."}`). `_UrllibTransport._read_json()` in `02_data/data/provider/fyers/live_auth.py` previously aborted on HTTP 308 without reading the body. Updated `_read_json()` to decode the JSON body on HTTP 308 if `Url`/`url` is present. Auth code extraction and token exchange now succeed completely.
2. Auto-Authentication Priority (`02_data/data/provider/factory.py`): In `_auto_authenticate()`, prioritized the fast, headless API-based `FyersAutoAuthEngine` (vagator API) before Chrome Selenium. When the full auto-login triple is present, authentication succeeds without opening Chrome or triggering anti-bot challenges. Selenium remains as fallback.
3. Credential Persistence & Reconnect UX (`00_app/app/headless.py`, `rust/vayren-shell/src/broker_connection.rs`, `shell.rs`, `main.rs`): Added `saved: bool` to `CredentialFieldView` in both Rust and Slint. Headless checks the OS vault and flags stored fields so the UI displays saved values (`ΓÇóΓÇóΓÇóΓÇóΓÇóΓÇóΓÇóΓÇó`) on load. Unchanged sentinels are not overwritten during connection requests. Added `submit_check()` on startup in `headless.py` so valid sessions are automatically validated and the broker shows `CONNECTED` on application launch without requiring re-entry.
4. Official Broker SVG Logos & UI Polish (`rust/vayren-shell/ui/`): Added pixel-perfect `zerodha.svg` and `fyers.svg` matching the user reference image (`media_1790134198935.png`). Added native `key-pressed` clipboard handling for Ctrl+C, Ctrl+V (`\u{16}`), Ctrl+X, Ctrl+A directly on `TextInput` and streamlined `[PASTE]` button to call `field-input.paste()` directly. Added "Account Connected & Verified" active status banner, capsule status pills, and prominent red `DISCONNECT BROKER` button.
5. Sidebar Status Synchronization (`rust/vayren-shell/src/shell.rs`, `main.rs`): Fixed UX confusion where the sidebar row for the selected broker remained `Connected` while the main card was in the `Authenticating` state. In `shell.rs`, the selected broker's sidebar status label, tone, and connected boolean are now derived directly from `workspace.state` (showing amber `Authenticating` during authentication, and green `Connected` only when verification succeeds).
6. Permanent Fix for Hang & Clear Real-Time Status View (`02_data/data/provider/fyers/`, `00_app/app/headless.py`, `rust/vayren-shell/`):
   - Diagnosed root cause of hang: The OS credential store had an accidental multiline prompt saved as `redirect_uri`, triggering FYERS HTTP 400 `redirectUrl mismatch`. `_read_json` previously suppressed the error body, causing an opaque fallback to Selenium (CAPTCHA) and `headless.py` waiting 120s in a loop.
   - Sanitization & Normalization (`credentials.py`, `headless.py`): Guaranteed `redirect_uri` always validates as a single-line HTTP/HTTPS URL, automatically falling back to `DEFAULT_REDIRECT_URL` (`http://127.0.0.1:9475/vayren/fyers-callback`). OS vault cleaned.
   - Fast Failure & Transparent Venue Errors (`live_auth.py`, `auto_auth.py`, `factory.py`): Decoded HTTPError JSON response body in `_read_json` to extract exact venue messages (`redirectUrl mismatch`), surfaced the message through `ensure_session`, and avoided doomed Selenium fallbacks on fatal credential/venue errors.
   - Clear Live UI Feedback (`ui/broker_connection.slint`, `broker_connection.rs`, `shell.rs`): Added active live authenticating feedback badge (`root.status-message`), dedicated right-panel Connection Status Feedback card showing real-time state/reason, and eliminated duplicate RETRY buttons on `is-failed`.
7. Verification: Live FYERS connection verified (`authenticated as XV29244`); 67/67 pytest tests passed (12/12 selenium + 55/55 broker); 9/9 broker_connection Rust tests passed; all governance validators (`validate_language_ownership.py`, `validate_authority.py`, `validate_routes.py`) passed (0 errors). Native binary compiled: `rust/target/debug/vayren-shell.exe`.

**Previous update (fyers-connect-end-to-end-fix, 2026-09-23):** VAYREN FYERS ΓÇö END-TO-END BROKER CONNECTION & AUTHENTICATION WIRING.
1. Root cause diagnosed: Slint "Connect to Fyers" button invoked `connect-requested`, which forwarded to `ui.on_broker_connect_requested` in `rust/vayren-shell/src/main.rs`, but `main.rs` only logged a stub `println!`; `python_bridge.rs` lacked `ConnectBroker`/`DisconnectBroker` commands in `BackendCommand`; and `00_app/app/headless.py` lacked `connect_broker`/`disconnect_broker` command handlers in its command loop. As a result, clicking "Connect to Fyers" never initiated authentication or reached `BrokerManager`.
2. Python Headless Backend Wiring (`00_app/app/headless.py`): Added `connect_broker` and `disconnect_broker` handlers. On `connect_broker`, non-empty UI credentials merge over existing OS vault stored credentials (defaulting `redirect_uri` to `http://127.0.0.1:9475/vayren/fyers-callback` if absent), `broker_manager.configure` saves them securely, and the backend awaits background worker thread settlement (or triggers `start_login` when manual login is required) before emitting the updated `system_snapshot`.
3. Rust Bridge & IPC Protocol (`rust/vayren-shell/src/python_bridge.rs`): Added `ConnectBroker { broker_id, credentials }` and `DisconnectBroker { broker_id }` to `BackendCommand` enum with full serialization unit tests.
4. Native Shell Asynchronous UI Wiring (`rust/vayren-shell/src/main.rs`): Upgraded `PythonBackend` handle to `Arc<PythonBackend>` for thread safety; wired `ui.on_broker_connect_requested`, `ui.on_broker_login_requested` (retry), and `ui.on_broker_disconnect_requested` to execute backend IPC in a background thread (`std::thread::spawn`), immediately updating Slint to the amber `Authenticating` state with step 2 active, and dispatching the final `BrokerWorkspace` projection back to the UI thread via `slint::invoke_from_event_loop`.
5. Real End-to-End Verification: Tested `connect_broker` through `app.headless` with real FYERS credentials. Confirmed that the existing `FyersSeleniumAuthEngine` launched Chrome, opened the live FYERS login page, successfully detected and handled the broker challenge ("FYERS shows a security check (captcha) at the login page"), and returned honest `LOGIN_REQUIRED` status with the exact broker reason.
